###############################################################################
# Death events: the end of days during the great plagues, and the quest for
# eternal life
#
# Part 1: The end of days (namespace eod)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: any independent player ruler whose realm the Nurgle plagues
# (Nurgle's Rot, the Creeping Buboes) or the Red Pox have reached.
#
# Also in this file (each part keeps its own header and namespace):
#   Part 2: The quest for eternal life and rebirth (namespace eternal)
#   Part 3: Mourning (namespace mourning)
#   Part 4: Suicide (namespace suicide)
#   Part 5: Chess with Death (namespace chess)
#   Part 6: Immortal rivals and the immortal crisis (namespace immortal)
#
# When the great plagues reach the realm, one of three chains runs (once
# per two years):
#   eod.1            Starter (on_yearly_pulse random list)
#   eod.2-21         Doomsayers, Morrslieb swallows the sun
#   eod.30-47        The Ashen Rider, a false prophet claiming to be the
#                    god reborn, who may raise a holy revolt
#   eod.50-62        The restless dead: rumours that the plague dead walk
#   eod.70           Clears the end_of_days flag
#
# Was events/rip_eod_events.txt. eod.1-21 are old RIP.14000-14020 (minus
# 13999), the Ashen Rider RIP.15099-15116 and the restless dead
# RIP.22000-22020, in order. eod.1 was gated on the vanilla bubonic plague,
# which the mod does not have.
###############################################################################

namespace = eod

# eod.1 End of days starts
# Hidden. When the Nurgle plagues or the Red Pox reach the realm, picks one of
# three chains: doomsayers (eod.2), the Ashen Rider (eod.30) or the restless
# dead (eod.50). Clears after two years (eod.70).
# Scope: ROOT is an independent player ruler. Fired from the on_yearly_pulse
# random list (was gated on the bubonic plague, which the mod does not have).
character_event = {
	id = eod.1
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	ai = no
	prisoner = no
	only_independent = yes

	trigger = {
		any_realm_province = {
			OR = { disease = nurgles_rot disease = creeping_buboes disease = typhus }
		}
		host = { character = ROOT }

		NOT = { has_character_flag = end_of_days }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = {
				narrative_event = { id = eod.2 } # Doomsayers
			}
			20 = {
				character_event = { id = eod.30 } # The Ashen Rider
			}
			20 = {
				narrative_event = { id = eod.50 } # The restless dead
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = end_of_days

		character_event = { id = eod.70 days = 730 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Doomsayers (eod.2-21)
###############################################################################

# eod.2 Doomsayers
# Doomsayers draw crowds in the capital. The ruler blames a rival, despairs,
# or lets it run its course. Greedy councillors may offer a scheme (eod.3).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.1.
narrative_event = {
	id = eod.2
	picture = GFX_evt_bring_out_your_dead
	title = EVTNAMEeod2
	desc = EVTDESCeod2
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = { save_event_target_as = capital }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod2_A
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				100 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							job_chancellor = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							job_marshal = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							job_treasurer = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							job_spymaster = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							job_spiritual = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
						}
					}
					job_chancellor = {
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							save_event_target_as = greedy_councillor
							ROOT = { set_character_flag = greedy_chancellor }
							character_event = { id = eod.4 }
							break = yes
						}
					}
					job_marshal = {
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							save_event_target_as = greedy_councillor
							ROOT = { set_character_flag = greedy_marshal }
							character_event = { id = eod.4 }
							break = yes
						}
					}
					job_treasurer = {
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							save_event_target_as = greedy_councillor
							ROOT = { set_character_flag = greedy_treasurer }
							character_event = { id = eod.4 }
							break = yes
						}
					}
					job_spymaster = {
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							save_event_target_as = greedy_councillor
							ROOT = { set_character_flag = greedy_spymaster }
							character_event = { id = eod.4 }
							break = yes
						}
					}
					job_spiritual = {
						if = {
							limit = { OR = { trait = greedy trait = deceitful } }
							save_event_target_as = greedy_councillor
							ROOT = { set_character_flag = greedy_spiritual }
							character_event = { id = eod.4 }
						}
					}
				}
				#Blame a rival
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { any_rival = { at_location = ROOT } } }
					random_rival = {
						limit = { at_location = ROOT }
						character_event = { id = eod.6 }
					}
				}
				#Become depressed
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOR = {
							trait = stressed
							trait = paranoid
							trait = craven
							is_weak_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = eod.5 }
				}
				#nothing special happens
				10 = { character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 20 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# eod.3 A greedy councillor
# A greedy or deceitful councillor offers to profit from the fear: make a deal
# or refuse.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.4.
character_event = {
	id = eod.3
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod3_A trigger = { has_character_flag = greedy_chancellor } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod3_B trigger = { has_character_flag = greedy_marshal } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod3_C trigger = { has_character_flag = greedy_treasurer } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod3_D trigger = { has_character_flag = greedy_spymaster } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod3_E trigger = { has_character_flag = greedy_spiritual } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#make a deal
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod3_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod3_A_TOOLTIP }
		clr_character_flag = greedy_chancellor
		clr_character_flag = greedy_marshal
		clr_character_flag = greedy_treasurer
		clr_character_flag = greedy_spymaster
		clr_character_flag = greedy_spiritual
		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = eod_deal_made
			character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 10 random = 20 }
		}
	}

	#dont take advantage of people
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod3_B
		piety = 100
		clr_character_flag = greedy_chancellor
		clr_character_flag = greedy_marshal
		clr_character_flag = greedy_treasurer
		clr_character_flag = greedy_spymaster
		clr_character_flag = greedy_spiritual
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 10 random = 20 } }
	}
}

# eod.4 Ping the greedy councillor
# Hidden. Sends the scheme back to the ruler (eod.3).
# Scope: ROOT is the councillor, FROM the ruler. Fired by eod.2.
character_event = {
	id = eod.4
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.3 days = 10 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# eod.5 Overcome with worry
# The ruler falls into despair and may turn depressed.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.2.
character_event = {
	id = eod.5
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = EVTDESCeod5

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod5_A
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } }
		add_trait = depressed
		set_character_flag = eod_depressed
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 10 random = 20 } }
	}
}

# eod.6 Ping the rival
# Hidden. The rival needles the ruler (eod.7).
# Scope: ROOT is the rival, FROM the ruler. Fired by eod.2.
character_event = {
	id = eod.6
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = rival
		FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.7 days = 10 random = 5 } }
	}
}

# eod.7 The rival annoys you
# The rival mocks the ruler in the crisis: forge evidence against them or rise
# above it.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.6.
character_event = {
	id = eod.7
	picture = GFX_evt_poke_king_in_chest
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	desc = EVTDESCeod7

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#forge some evidence
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod7_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod7_A_TOOLTIP }
		piety = -100
		set_character_flag = eod_rival
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 10 random = 20 } }
	}

	#grow up
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod7_B
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.11 days = 10 random = 20 } }
	}
}

# eod.11 Morrslieb swallows the sun
# The Chaos moon eclipses the sun. The ruler scams the people, sends a mob
# after a rival, preaches repentance, despairs, or gives hope.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the doomsayer chain (eod.2-7).
character_event = {
	id = eod.11
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = EVTDESCeod11

	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Scam the people
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod11_A
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = eod_deal_made
			NOT = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod11_A_TOOLTIP }
		clr_character_flag = eod_deal_made
		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:greedy_councillor = { character_event = { id = eod.12 } } }
	}

	#Send mob after rival
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod11_B
		trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_rival NOT = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod11_B_TOOLTIP }
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:rival = { character_event = { id = eod.14 days = 5 random = 2 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod11_C
		trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed }
		add_character_modifier = { name = bad_crisis_management years = 10 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod11_D
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod11_D_TOOLTIP }
		set_character_flag = eod_pious
		piety = 100
		add_character_modifier = { name = church_appreciates years = 20 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod11_E
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed } }
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod11_E_TOOLTIP }
		set_character_flag = eod_diplomat
		prestige = 100
		add_character_modifier = { name = confident years = 10 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}
}

# eod.12 Ping the greedy councillor
# Hidden. The councillor collects from the frightened people (eod.13).
# Scope: ROOT is the councillor, FROM the ruler. Fired by eod.11.
character_event = {
	id = eod.12
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.13 days = 7 } }
	}
}

# eod.13 The councillor brings the profits
# The scam pays out: accept the money.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.12.
character_event = {
	id = eod.13
	picture = GFX_evt_bribe_accepted
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	desc = EVTDESCeod13

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			33 = { set_character_flag = earnings_1 }
			33 = { set_character_flag = earnings_2 }
			33 = { set_character_flag = earnings_3 }
		}
	}

	#accept
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod13_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod13_A_TOOLTIP }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = earnings_1 }
			scaled_wealth = 0.5
			clr_character_flag = earnings_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = earnings_2 }
			scaled_wealth = 1
			clr_character_flag = earnings_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = earnings_3 }
			scaled_wealth = 1.5
			clr_character_flag = earnings_3
		}
		set_character_flag = eod_scam
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}
}

# eod.14 The mob goes after the rival
# The desperate mob hunts the rival. They may find out who set them on
# (eod.15).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.11.
character_event = {
	id = eod.14
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	desc = EVTDESCeod14

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#uh oh
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod13_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod13_A_TOOLTIP }
		random_list = {
			33 = {
				FROM = {
					set_character_flag = eod_rival_dies
					character_event = { id = eod.16 days = 3 random = 3 }
				}
				hidden_tooltip = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM } }
			}
			33 = {
				hidden_tooltip = {
					FROM = {
						set_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed
						character_event = { id = eod.16 days = 3 random = 3 }
					}
				}
				add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			}
			33 = {
				hidden_tooltip = {
					FROM = {
						set_character_flag = eod_mob_failed
						character_event = { id = eod.16 days = 3 random = 3 }
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = eod.15 days = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# eod.15 The rival finds the culprit
# The rival learns the ruler sent the mob.
# Scope: ROOT is the rival. Fired by eod.14.
character_event = {
	id = eod.15
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	desc = EVTDESCeod15

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#that snake
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod15_A
		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_charge_slanderer years = 10 }
	}
}

# eod.16 The rival against the mob
# How the rival fared against the mob.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.14.
character_event = {
	id = eod.16
	picture = GFX_evt_hiding_from_pursuers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod16_A trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_rival_dies } }

	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod16_B trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed } }

	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod16_C trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_mob_failed } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#everyone gets what they deserve
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod16_A
		trigger = {
			OR = { has_character_flag = eod_rival_dies has_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}

	#not good
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsymptom_alas
		trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_mob_failed }
		tooltip = {
			reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_charge_slanderer years = 10 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.21 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}
}

# eod.21 Better days
# The doomsayer chain ends. The outcome text follows what the ruler did.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the doomsayer chain.
narrative_event = {
	id = eod.21
	picture = GFX_evt_depopulated_town
	title = EVTNAMEeod21
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	#scam outcome
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod21_A trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_scam } }

	#Rival blamed and hurt
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeod21_B
		trigger = {
			OR = { has_character_flag = eod_rival_dies has_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed }
		}
	}

	#Rival blamed but lived
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod21_C trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_mob_failed } }

	#live pure lives
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod21_D trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_pious } }

	#disappointed in ruler
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod21_E trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_depressed } }

	#disappointed in ruler
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeod21_F trigger = { has_character_flag = eod_diplomat } }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad } }
			set_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad
		}

		clr_character_flag = end_of_days
	}

	#normal life resumes
	option = {
		name = EXCELLENT
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = eod_scam
				has_character_flag = eod_pious
				has_character_flag = eod_diplomat
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_dies
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed
		clr_character_flag = eod_mob_failed
		clr_character_flag = eod_scam
		clr_character_flag = eod_pious
		clr_character_flag = eod_depressed
		clr_character_flag = eod_diplomat
	}

	#rival hurt
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod21_A
		trigger = {
			OR = { has_character_flag = eod_rival_dies has_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed }
		}
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_dies
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed
		clr_character_flag = eod_mob_failed
		clr_character_flag = eod_scam
		clr_character_flag = eod_pious
		clr_character_flag = eod_depressed
		clr_character_flag = eod_diplomat
	}

	#rival unhurt
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod21_B
		trigger = {
			OR = { has_character_flag = eod_mob_failed has_character_flag = eod_depressed }
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = vicious_rumors years = 10 }
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_dies
		clr_character_flag = eod_rival_maimed
		clr_character_flag = eod_mob_failed
		clr_character_flag = eod_scam
		clr_character_flag = eod_pious
		clr_character_flag = eod_depressed
		clr_character_flag = eod_diplomat
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The Ashen Rider (eod.30-47)
###############################################################################

# eod.30 The Ashen Rider is chosen
# Hidden. Picks or creates a zealous courtier to become the false prophet.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.1.
character_event = {
	id = eod.30
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_courtier = { can_be_prophet_of_doom_trigger = yes } }

			random_courtier = {
				limit = { can_be_prophet_of_doom_trigger = yes }
				hidden_tooltip = {
					add_trait = adventurer
					change_diplomacy = 10
					change_martial = 7
					change_learning = 5
					health = 3
				}
				save_event_target_as = EoD_doomsday_prophet
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { any_courtier = { can_be_prophet_of_doom_trigger = yes } } }
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						age = 36
						female = yes
						random_traits = yes
						health = 5
						attributes = { diplomacy = 10 martial = 7 learning = 5 }
						trait = adventurer
						trait = zealous
						trait = fair
						religion = ROOT
						dynasty = random
						race = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
					}
					new_character = {
						# Set Racial Trait
						character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
						remove_trait = content
						remove_trait = slow
						remove_trait = imbecile
						remove_trait = ugly
						save_event_target_as = EoD_doomsday_prophet
					}
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						age = 36
						random_traits = yes
						health = 5
						attributes = { diplomacy = 10 martial = 7 learning = 5 }
						trait = adventurer
						trait = zealous
						trait = fair
						religion = ROOT
						dynasty = random
						race = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
					}
					new_character = {
						# Set Racial Trait
						character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
						remove_trait = content
						remove_trait = slow
						remove_trait = imbecile
						remove_trait = ugly
						save_event_target_as = EoD_doomsday_prophet
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		character_event = { id = eod.31 }
	}
}

# eod.31 The Ashen Rider
# A noble claims to be the god reborn and gathers followers for a war that
# will burn the plague out of the land.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.30.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeod31
	id = eod.31
	desc = EVTDESCeod31
	picture = GFX_evt_depopulated_town
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_from = yes
	hide_new = yes

	#Deal with it
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod31_A
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.32 days = 20 random = 10 } }
	}
}

# eod.32 The rumours reach court
# The court talks of salvation through fire: mock the prophet, warn the
# people, or scare them.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.31.
character_event = {
	id = eod.32
	desc = EVTDESCeod32
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { any_friend = { is_adult = yes } }

			random_friend = {
				limit = { is_adult = yes }
				save_event_target_as = EoD_worried_one
				set_character_flag = worried_friend
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { any_friend = { is_adult = yes } }
				any_playable_ruler = { ai = yes is_adult = yes NOT = { is_rival = ROOT } }
			}

			random_independent_ruler = {
				limit = {
					ai = yes
					is_adult = yes
					is_landed = yes
					NOT = { is_rival = ROOT }
					NOT = { distance = { value = 200 who = ROOT } }
				}
				save_event_target_as = EoD_worried_one
				set_character_flag = worried_ruler
			}
		}
	}

	#Make fun of horses
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod32_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = gave_funny_speech duration = 200 }

		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { character_event = { id = eod.33 } } }
	}

	#Give a speech of warning
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod32_B

		add_character_modifier = { name = gave_pious_speech duration = 200 }

		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { character_event = { id = eod.33 } } }
	}

	#Give a scary speech
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod32_C

		add_character_modifier = { name = gave_scary_speech duration = 200 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:EoD_worried_one = {
				clr_character_flag = worried_friend
				clr_character_flag = worried_ruler
				clear_event_target = EoD_worried_one
			}

			if = {
				limit = { any_rival = { is_adult = yes } }

				random_rival = {
					limit = { is_adult = yes }
					save_event_target_as = EoD_worried_one
					set_character_flag = worried_rival
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { any_rival = { is_adult = yes } }
					any_playable_ruler = { ai = yes is_adult = yes }
				}

				random_independent_ruler = {
					limit = {
						ai = yes
						is_adult = yes
						is_landed = yes
						NOT = { distance = { value = 200 where = ROOT } }
					}
					save_event_target_as = EoD_worried_one
					set_character_flag = worried_ruler
				}
			}
			event_target:EoD_worried_one = { character_event = { id = eod.33 } }
		}
	}
}

# eod.33 A worried ruler
# Hidden. A neighbouring ruler writes about the prophet (eod.34).
# Scope: ROOT is the other ruler, FROM the ruler. Fired by eod.32.
letter_event = {
	id = eod.33

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_alive = yes
	}

	#I should write a letter expressing my concern!
	option = {
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.34 days = 10 } } }
	}
}

# eod.34 A worried letter
# A neighbour writes with concern about the Ashen Rider.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the writer. Fired by eod.33.
letter_event = {
	id = eod.34

	immediate = {
		any_realm_province = {
			limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = "religious_tension" duration = 365 }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeod34_A
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_friend } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeod34_B
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_ruler } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeod34_C
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_rival } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Oh dear
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod34_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod34_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.35 days = 30 } }
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_friend } }
	}
	#Very well
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod34_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod34_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.35 days = 30 } }
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_ruler } }
	}

	#That jackal!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod34_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod34_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.35 days = 30 } }
		trigger = { event_target:EoD_worried_one = { has_character_flag = worried_rival } }
	}
}

# eod.35 Troops at the gates
# The Ashen Rider's host gathers outside: parley or fight.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.34.
character_event = {
	id = eod.35
	desc = EVTDESCeod35
	picture = GFX_evt_large_army
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		capital_scope = {
			county = {
				hidden_tooltip = {
					event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
						create_title = {
							tier = DUKE
							landless = yes
							temporary = yes
							adventurer = yes
							culture = ROOT
							name = "EoD_UPRISING"
							holder = THIS
						}
						wealth = 100

						spawn_unit = {
							owner = THIS
							province = PREVPREV
							home = THIS
							match_character = ROOT
							match_mult = 0.5
							match_min = 300
							match_max = 5000
							attrition = 1.0
							earmark = "EoD_uprising_troops"
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Parlay
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod35_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod35_A_TOOLTIP }
		Piety = 100

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.36 days = 10 } }
	}

	#Fight
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod35_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod35_B_TOOLTIP }
		Prestige = 100

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.43 } }
	}
}

# eod.36 The Ashen Rider unmasked
# The prophet is brought before the ruler: bribe, imprison or execute them.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.35.
character_event = {
	id = eod.36
	desc = EVTDESCeod36
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_demesne_title = {
			limit = { tier = COUNT can_be_given_away = yes location = { is_capital = no } }
			save_event_target_as = EoD_some_province
		}
	}

	#BRIBE prophet
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod36_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod36_A_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			random = {
				chance = 25
				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.258 } } #Notify Cynical
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = religious_tension
			}
			event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
				remove_opinion = { modifier = opinion_evil_tyrant who = ROOT }
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					#prophet abdicates
					letter_event = { id = eod.37 days = 50 }
				}
				33 = {
					#prophet kill themselves
					character_event = { id = eod.45 days = 30 }
				}
				33 = {
					#prophet is readjusted to society
					character_event = { id = eod.44 days = 50 }
				}
			}
		}

		event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
			grant_title = event_target:EoD_some_province
			set_defacto_liege = ROOT
			disband_event_forces = EoD_uprising_troops
		}
	}

	#IMPRISON prophet
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod36_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod36_B_TOOLTIP }
		Prestige = 50

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
				imprison = ROOT
				disband_event_forces = EoD_uprising_troops
			}
			random_list = {
				#they escape from prison
				50 = { character_event = { id = eod.42 days = 20 } }
				#they kill themselves
				50 = {
					set_character_flag = imprisoned_white_rider
					character_event = { id = eod.45 days = 20 }
				}
			}
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = religious_tension
			}
		}
	}

	#EXECUTE prophet
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod36_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod36_C_TOOLTIP }
		Piety = 50

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#they die
				40 = {
					event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
						death = { death_reason = death_execution_burning KILLER = FROM }
					}
				}

				#they escape execution
				60 = {
					#botched execution
					character_event = { id = eod.40 }

					event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_survivor

						add_character_modifier = { name = escaped_execution duration = -1 }
						leave_court_effect = yes
					}
				}
			}
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				remove_province_modifier = religious_tension

				add_province_modifier = { name = "ordered_our_prophet_executed" duration = 365 }
			}
			disband_event_forces = EoD_uprising_troops
		}
	}
}

# eod.37 The prophet abdicates
# The bribed prophet gives up and sends a farewell note.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.36.
letter_event = {
	id = eod.37
	desc = EVTDESCeod37

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
					holder_scope = { character = event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet }
				}
			}
			grant_title_no_opinion = ROOT
		}

		event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = { leave_court_effect = yes }

		if = {
			limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad } }
			set_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad
		}
	}

	#I see
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod37_A
		clr_character_flag = end_of_days
	}
}

# eod.43 The zealots revolt
# Hidden. The prophet's followers rise as a holy revolt. The war ends in
# eod.46 or eod.47.
# Scope: ROOT is the prophet, FROM the ruler. Fired by eod.35.
character_event = {
	id = eod.43

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		primary_title = { add_claim = event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet }
		event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
			set_character_flag = EoD_prophet_attacker
			war = { target = ROOT casus_belli = claim thirdparty_title = PREV }
		}
	}

	#Oh noes!
	option = { }
}

# eod.44 The prophet settles in
# The bribed prophet lives quietly at court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.36.
character_event = {
	id = eod.44
	desc = EVTDESCeod44
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
			OR = { is_councillor = yes is_ruler = yes has_minor_title = yes }
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad } }
			set_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad
		}
	}

	#I'm impressed
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod44_A

		clr_character_flag = end_of_days

		add_friend = event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet

		event_target:EoD_doomsday_prophet = {
			add_character_modifier = { name = friends_in_high_places duration = 365 }
		}
	}

	#I'll never trust the Ashen Rider
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod44_B
	}
}

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { disband_event_forces = EoD_uprising_troops } }
	}
}

# eod.50 Days of the restless dead
# Rumours spread that the plague dead rise from the pits. Starts the peasant
# chain (51-61).

	picture = GFX_evt_plague_spreading

	is_triggered_only = yes

	}
}

# eod.51 Peasants at the gates
# Frightened peasants gather outside the gates.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.50.
character_event = {
	id = eod.51
	desc = EVTDESCeod51
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Invite the leader
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod51_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod51_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			create_character = {
				female = no
				age = 35
				race = ROOT
				culture = ROOT
				religion = ROOT
				trait = disfigured
				trait = paranoid
				trait = wroth
				trait = stubborn
				trait = gregarious
				trait = peasant_leader
				trait = tough_soldier
				dynasty = none
			}

			new_character = {
				# Set Racial Trait
				character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
				character_event = { id = eod.52 days = 7 }
				save_global_event_target_as = end_peasant_leader
			}

			set_character_flag = exposed_to_outside
		}
	}

	#Keep the gates closed
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod51_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod51_B_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_unrest years = 2 }
			}

			event_target:end_peasant_leader = { leave_court_effect = yes }

			#Ending
			random_list = {
				33 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.59 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				33 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				33 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# eod.52 Ping the peasant leader

	}
}

# eod.53 The peasant leader demands
# The leader wants the dead hunted: give levies, blame him, or talk him round.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the peasant leader. Fired by eod.52.
character_event = {
	id = eod.53
	desc = EVTDESCeod53
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Heal them
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod53_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod53_A_TOOLTIP }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -1 min = -150 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Good ending or death
			random_list = {
				30 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				70 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Teach him about what the holy scripture really says, turn him into a prophet
	option = {
		trigger = { learning = 12 }
		tooltip_info = learning
		name = EVTOPTeod53_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod53_B_TOOLTIP }
		FROM = { add_trait = zealous }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.62 days = 110 random = 20 } } }
	}

	#Kill them
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod53_C

		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = eod.54 days = 110 random = 20 }

			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = hunting_dead years = 1 }
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod53_C_TOOLTIP }
	}

	#Convince him to work for you
	option = {
		trigger = { diplomacy = 12 }
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		name = EVTOPTeod53_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod53_D_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eod.54 days = 110 random = 20 } }

		FROM = { add_trait = brave }
	}
}

# eod.54 A mother and child
# Hidden. Creates the mother whose sick child was slain in the hunt.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.53.
character_event = {
	id = eod.54

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		create_character = {
			female = yes
			age = 29
			race = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			religion = ROOT
			dynasty = none
			random_traits = yes
			trait = depressed
		}

		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			remove_trait = cruel
			remove_trait = arbitrary
			save_event_target_as = end_mother
			FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.55 } }
		}

		create_character = {
			age = 10
			race = ROOT
			culture = ROOT
			religion = ROOT
			dynasty = none
			trait = curious
			trait = affectionate
		}

		new_character = {
			# Set Racial Trait
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
			set_mother = event_target:end_mother
			save_global_event_target_as = end_child
			death = { death_reason = death_execution_beheading killer = FROM }
		}
	}
}

# eod.55 A grieving mother
# A mother whose sick child was killed by the hunters wants the ruler's
# apology.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.54.
character_event = {
	id = eod.55
	desc = EVTDESCeod55
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_peasants

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Apologize and give money
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod55_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod55_A_TOOLTIP }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.1 min = -25 }
		prestige = -200

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Good ending or death
			random_list = {
				33 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				67 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Blame it on the leader
	option = {
		trigger = { intrigue = 12 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue

		name = EVTOPTeod55_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod55_B_TOOLTIP }

		event_target:end_peasant_leader = { imprison = yes }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Death or good ending
			random_list = {
				20 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				80 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Send her away
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod55_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod55_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:end_mother = { leave_court_effect = yes } }

		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = kind } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = { limit = { trait = kind } remove_trait = kind }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_crisis years = 3 }
			}

			#Revolt or good ending
			random_list = {
				80 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.59 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				20 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# eod.62 Ping the preacher
# Hidden. The peasant leader starts preaching (eod.56).

# chain.
character_event = {

		FROM = { character_event = { id = eod.56 } }
	}
}

# eod.56 The preacher
# The peasant leader preaches about the dead: have him killed or let him be.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eod.62.
character_event = {
	id = eod.56
	desc = EVTDESCeod56
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_heretic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Arrest him
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod56_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod56_A_TOOLTIP }

		FROM = { imprison = yes }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }
				add_province_modifier = { name = peasant_crisis years = 3 }
			}

			#Revolt, death or good ending
			random_list = {
				60 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.59 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				20 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				20 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Assassinate
	option = {
		trigger = { intrigue = 12 }
		tooltip_info = intrigue

		name = EVTOPTeod56_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod56_B_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#Good outcome
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { intrigue = 13 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { intrigue = 19 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { intrigue = 22 } }

					character_event = { id = eod.57 days = 20 }
				}
				#Bad outcome
				50 = { character_event = { id = eod.58 days = 20 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Let it be
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod56_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod56_C_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#Death or good ending
			random_list = {
				60 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.60 days = 110 random = 20 } }
				40 = { narrative_event = { id = eod.61 days = 110 random = 20 } }
			}
			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = {
							has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
							has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
							has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
						}
					}
					add_province_modifier = { name = depopulated_1 duration = -1 }
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_province_modifier = depopulated_1 }
					add_province_modifier = { name = depopulated_2 duration = -1 }
					break = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_province_modifier = depopulated_2 }
					add_province_modifier = { name = depopulated_3 duration = -1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# eod.61 A glimmer of hope
# Good end: the rumours die down and the worst has passed.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the peasant chain.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeod61
	id = eod.61
	desc = EVTDESCeod61
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_bishop

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { ai = no NOT = { has_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad } }
			set_character_flag = achievement_not_so_bad
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeod61_A

		capital_scope = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_province_modifier = peasant_unrest }
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod61_A_TOOLTIP_A }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_province_modifier = peasant_crisis }
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeod61_A_TOOLTIP_B }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = end_of_days

			any_realm_province = {
				limit = { owner = { character = ROOT } }

				if = {
					limit = { has_province_modifier = peasant_unrest }
					remove_province_modifier = peasant_unrest
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_province_modifier = peasant_crisis }
					remove_province_modifier = peasant_crisis
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 2: The quest for eternal life and rebirth (namespace eternal)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: an ageing human (not dwarf or elf) playable ruler who is not
# already immortal; once per life.
#
# A ruler who fears death sends a councillor to find a mystic. The mystic
# follows the councillor who found her, and most are frauds:
#   eternal.1-22     The search (eternal.1 on the ten-year pulse)
#   eternal.101-127  A Norscan seeress (chancellor, diplomacy): a feast of
#                    warriors, the rockfall, the tree of youth
#   eternal.201-228  An Araby marid-shaman (marshal, martial): ancestor
#                    sacrifice, the serpent, the spirit draught
#   eternal.301-327  A Strigany alchemist (spymaster, intrigue): the
#                    pickpocket, the method, the Philosopher's Stone
#   eternal.401-428  A Nehekharan child (chaplain, learning): Usirian's
#                    scales, the fast, loosing the soul
#   eternal.500-510  The court investigates the mystic
#   eternal.600      Reincarnation copies traits to the newborn
#   eternal.700-704  A dying parent says the heir is just like their
#                    grandfather (on_death)
#   eternal.705-706  An ancestor reborn in a newborn, for faiths that hold
#                    the soul is born again among kin, the Dawi among
#                    them (on_birth)
#
# Was events/rip_eternal_life_events.txt (RIP.21000-21509 and RIP.21991).
# eternal.N is old RIP.(20999+N), and eternal.600 is RIP.21991. eternal.1
# was a 3000-month MTTH and is now on the ten-year (decade) pulse
# (eternal_life_quest list in wh_death_on_actions.txt).
###############################################################################

namespace = eternal

###############################################################################
# The search (eternal.1-22)
###############################################################################

# eternal.1 Matters of life and death
# An ageing human ruler starts to fear death and turns to the council, or to
# loneliness. Starts the eternal life quest.
# Scope: ROOT is a playable ruler. Fired from the on_decade_pulse
# eternal_life_quest list (was a 3000-month MTTH). Dwarfs, elves and the
# immortal never get it.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal1
	id = eternal.1
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_danse_macabre

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse, eternal_life_quest list
	only_playable = yes
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no
	min_age = 16

	hide_from = yes
	hide_new = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_events }
		is_inaccessible_trigger = no
		immortal = no
		NOT = { is_immortal_trigger = yes }
		NOR = { trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_elf }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 has_disease_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 NOT = { health = 2 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 age = 50 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 age = 70 }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = eternal_life_events

		if = {
			limit = { is_married = no NOT = { trait = ambitious } }

			set_character_flag = eternal1_lonely

			if = {
				limit = {
					any_courtier = {
						sad_example_trigger = yes
						is_inaccessible_trigger = no
						ai = yes
					}
				}

				random_courtier = {
					limit = { sad_example_trigger = yes is_inaccessible_trigger = no ai = yes }

					add_trait = depressed
					save_event_target_as = eternal_life_sad_example
				}
			}

			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { any_courtier = { sad_example_trigger = yes } } }

				create_character = {
					age = 62
					random_traits = yes
					trait = depressed
					religion = ROOT
					dynasty = random
					race = ROOT
					culture = ROOT
				}
				new_character = {
					save_event_target_as = eternal_life_sad_example
					# Set Racial Trait
					character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal1_MARRIED
		trigger = { AND = { is_married = yes any_spouse = { is_lover = ROOT } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCeternal1_UNMARRIED trigger = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely } }

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal1_UNMARRIED_AMBITIOUS
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				AND = { is_married = no trait = ambitious }
				AND = {
					is_married = yes
					NOT = { trait = kind }
					NOT = { any_spouse = { is_lover = ROOT } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal1_MARRIED_NO_LOVE
		trigger = {
			AND = { is_married = yes trait = kind NOT = { any_spouse = { is_lover = ROOT } } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal1_FALLBACK
		trigger = {
			is_married = no
			NOT = { trait = ambitious }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely }
		}
	}

	#Summon the council!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal1_A
		narrative_event = { id = eternal.2 days = 10 tooltip = EVTOPTeternal1_A_TOOLTIP }

		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely } }
	}

	#Summon the council! Who cares about lonely people?
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal1_B
		narrative_event = { id = eternal.2 days = 15 tooltip = EVTOPTeternal1_B_TOOLTIP }

		trigger = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely }

		clr_character_flag = eternal1_lonely
	}

	#Nah
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal1_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal1_C_TOOLTIP }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }

		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely } }
	}

	#Nah, I wanna spend more time with the sad person
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal1_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal1_D_TOOLTIP }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = content } } add_trait = content }

		event_target:eternal_life_sad_example = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_budding_friendship }
			hidden_tooltip = { add_friend = FROM }
		}

		trigger = { has_character_flag = eternal1_lonely }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = eternal_life_sad_example
			clr_character_flag = eternal1_lonely
		}
	}
}

# eternal.2 A meeting of the minds
# The council is summoned: send the chancellor, marshal, spymaster or chaplain
# to find a mystic, or brood.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eternal.1.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal2
	id = eternal.2
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal2
		picture = GFX_evt_council_arabic
		trigger = { culture_group = arabic }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal2
		picture = GFX_evt_council
		trigger = { NOT = { culture_group = arabic } }
	}
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_A
		trigger = { diplomacy = 12 job_chancellor = { always = yes } }

		tooltip_info = diplomacy

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_A_TOOLTIP }

		job_chancellor = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_advisor
				character_event = { id = eternal.3 days = 200 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eternal.22 days = 600 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_B
		trigger = { martial = 12 job_marshal = { always = yes } }

		tooltip_info = martial

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_B_TOOLTIP }

		job_marshal = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_advisor
				character_event = { id = eternal.3 days = 200 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eternal.22 days = 600 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_C
		trigger = { intrigue = 12 job_spymaster = { always = yes } }

		tooltip_info = intrigue

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_C_TOOLTIP }

		job_spymaster = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_advisor
				character_event = { id = eternal.3 days = 200 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eternal.22 days = 600 } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_D
		trigger = { learning = 12 job_spiritual = { always = yes } }

		tooltip_info = learning

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_D_TOOLTIP }

		job_spiritual = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT }

			hidden_tooltip = {
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_advisor
				character_event = { id = eternal.3 days = 200 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = eternal.22 days = 600 } }
	}

	#Dumb option
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_E
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOR = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 12 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
				AND = {
					is_dumb_trigger = yes
					NOT = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 12 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
				}
			}
		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_E_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				85 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.7 days = 150 } #Fail
				}
				15 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.15 days = 365 random = 60 } #Stranger appears on her own
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Neutral fallback
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal2_F
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { diplomacy = 12 }
				NOT = { martial = 12 }
				NOT = { intrigue = 12 }
				NOT = { learning = 12 }
			}
			OR = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 12 intrigue = 12 learning = 12 }
			is_dumb_trigger = NO
		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal2_F_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.8 days = 150 } #Fail
				}
				35 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.15 days = 365 random = 60 } #Stranger appears on her own
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.3 The advisor searches
# Hidden. The chosen advisor reports back (eternal.9-12).
# Scope: ROOT is the advisor, FROM the ruler. Fired by eternal.2.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.3

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_job_title = job_chancellor }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.9 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_job_title = job_marshal }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.10 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_job_title = job_spymaster }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.11 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_job_title = job_spiritual }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.12 } }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.13 The advisor keeps looking
# Hidden. After the answer, the advisor finds a mystic (eternal.14) or nothing
# (eternal.18).
# Scope: ROOT is the advisor, FROM the ruler. Fired by eternal.9-12.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.13

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				#finds nothing
				FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.18 days = 150 } }
			}
			50 = {
				#finds a mystic
				modifier = { factor = 4 has_character_flag = eternal_life_expensive_option }

				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = eternal_life_affordable_option }

				FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.14 days = 300 } }
				clr_character_flag = eternal_life_expensive_option
				clr_character_flag = eternal_life_affordable_option
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.14 A mystic appears
# The advisor has found a mystic: hear her out or send her away. Her kind
# follows the advisor: a Norscan seeress (diplomacy), an Araby marid-shaman
# (martial), a Strigany alchemist (intrigue) or a Nehekharan child (learning).
# Most are frauds.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the advisor. Fired by eternal.13.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal14
	id = eternal.14
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	trigger = {
		FROM = { is_alive = yes liege = { character = ROOT } }
		prisoner = no
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.14 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal14_CHANCELLOR
		picture = GFX_evt_emissary
		trigger = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_chancellor } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal14_MARSHAL
		picture = GFX_evt_emissary
		trigger = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_marshal } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal14_SPYMASTER
		picture = GFX_evt_whispers
		trigger = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal14_SPIRITUAL
		picture = GFX_evt_emissary
		trigger = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_spiritual } }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_chancellor } }
			set_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_marshal } }
			set_character_flag = martial_mystic
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_spymaster } }
			set_character_flag = intrigue_mystic
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { has_job_title = job_spiritual } }
			set_character_flag = learning_mystic
		}

		random_playable_ruler = {
			limit = {
				is_within_diplo_range = ROOT
				has_job_title = job_chancellor
				any_child = { is_female = no }
			}
			save_event_target_as = eternal_life_random_diplomat
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal14_A
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				name = "Idunn"
				dna = chaehghfacw
				dynasty = none
				religion = norscan_gods
				culture = fjarmann
				female = yes
				age = 17
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = grey_eminence
				trait = gregarious
				trait = mystic
				trait = lustful
				trait = charitable
				trait = fair
				trait = trusting
				trait = poet
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_youthful
				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 22 } }

						change_diplomacy = 6
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = martial_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = atruhayid
				culture = tevari
				female = yes
				age = 36
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = barbarian_4
				trait = mystic
				trait = robust
				trait = wroth
				trait = diligent
				trait = proud
				trait = brave
				trait = aggressive_leader
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_fearless

				random_list = {
					50 = { add_scarred_high_effect = yes }
					50 = { add_trait = disfigured }
				}

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 22 } }

						change_martial = 6
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = intrigue_mystic }
			create_character = {
				name = "Cleopatra"
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = vampiric
				culture = strigany
				female = yes
				age = 72
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = elusive_shadow
				trait = mystic
				trait = shrewd
				trait = diligent
				trait = proud
				trait = paranoid
				trait = ambitious
				trait = deceitful
				trait = erudite
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_alchemist

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 22 } }

						change_intrigue = 6
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = learning_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = nehekharan_gods
				culture = nehekka
				female = yes
				age = 9
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = mystic
				trait = quick
				trait = fussy
				trait = conscientious
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_gentle
				change_diplomacy = 2
				change_stewardship = 2
				change_intrigue = -1

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 22 } }

						change_learning = 15
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic
		clr_character_flag = martial_mystic
		clr_character_flag = intrigue_mystic
		clr_character_flag = learning_mystic

		event_target:eternal_life_mystic = {
			narrative_event = { id = eternal.19 days = 30 random = 20 }
		}
		save_event_target_as = eternal_life_seeker
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal14_B
		clr_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic
		clr_character_flag = martial_mystic
		clr_character_flag = intrigue_mystic
		clr_character_flag = learning_mystic
	}
}

# eternal.22 A mystic finds you
# Hidden. If the advisor died during the search, a mystic may still turn up
# (eternal.15).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eternal.2 after 600 days.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.22

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			event_target:eternal_life_advisor = { is_alive = no }
			event_target:eternal_life_advisor = { NOT = { liege = { character = ROOT } } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.8 } #Fail
				}
				35 = {
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.15 } #Stranger appears on her own
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.15 Heeding the call
# A hooded stranger asks to see the ruler alone.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eternal.22 and the advisor chain.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal15
	id = eternal.15
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	desc = EVTDESCeternal15
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = { set_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic }
			25 = { set_character_flag = martial_mystic }
			25 = { set_character_flag = intrigue_mystic }
			25 = { set_character_flag = learning_mystic }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal15_A
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				name = "Idunn"
				dna = chaehghfacw
				dynasty = none
				religion = norscan_gods
				culture = fjarmann
				female = yes
				age = 17
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = grey_eminence
				trait = gregarious
				trait = mystic
				trait = lustful
				trait = fair
				trait = trusting
				trait = poet
				trait = celibate
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_youthful
				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { diplomacy = 22 } }
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
				change_diplomacy = 6
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = martial_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = atruhayid
				culture = tevari
				female = yes
				age = 36
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = barbarian_4
				trait = mystic
				trait = robust
				trait = wroth
				trait = diligent
				trait = proud
				trait = brave
				trait = desert_terrain_leader
				trait = aggressive_leader
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_fearless
				add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes

				random_list = {
					50 = { add_scarred_medium_effect = yes }
					50 = { add_trait = disfigured }
				}

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { martial = 22 } }

						change_martial = 6
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = intrigue_mystic }
			create_character = {
				name = "Cleopatra"
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = vampiric
				culture = strigany
				female = yes
				age = 72
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = elusive_shadow
				trait = mystic
				trait = shrewd
				trait = diligent
				trait = proud
				trait = paranoid
				trait = ambitious
				trait = deceitful
				trait = erudite
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_alchemist

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { intrigue = 22 } }

						change_intrigue = 6
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = learning_mystic }
			create_character = {
				random_traits = no
				dynasty = none
				religion = nehekharan_gods
				culture = nehekka
				female = yes
				age = 9
				attributes = { learning = 10 }
				health = 15
				trait = creature_human
				trait = mystic
				trait = quick
				trait = fussy
				trait = conscientious
			}
			new_character = {
				set_character_flag = eternal_life_mystic

				will_not_marry_effect = yes

				give_nickname = nick_the_gentle
				change_diplomacy = 2
				change_stewardship = 2
				change_intrigue = -1

				random_list = {
					90 = { set_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
					10 = {
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 10 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 13 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 16 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 19 } }
						modifier = { factor = 1.4 FROM = { learning = 22 } }

						change_learning = 15
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
		clr_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic
		clr_character_flag = martial_mystic
		clr_character_flag = intrigue_mystic
		clr_character_flag = learning_mystic

		event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.19 } }
		save_event_target_as = eternal_life_seeker
		log = "mystic recieves 18"
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal15_B
		clr_character_flag = diplomacy_mystic
		clr_character_flag = martial_mystic
		clr_character_flag = intrigue_mystic
		clr_character_flag = learning_mystic
	}
}

# eternal.19 The mystic introduces herself
# Hidden. Prompts the mystic to meet the ruler (eternal.20).
# Scope: ROOT is the mystic. Fired by eternal.14 and eternal.15.
narrative_event = {
	id = eternal.19

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.20 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# eternal.20 An intriguing encounter
# The mystic offers her quest: accept, keep her at court, send her away, or
# (zealots) imprison the heretic.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by eternal.19.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal20
	id = eternal.20
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_emissary

	#Diplomacy mystic, same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_DIPLOMACY_A
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = grey_eminence religion = ROOT } }
	}
	#Martial mystic, same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_MARTIAL_A
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = barbarian_4 religion = ROOT } }
	}
	#Intrigue mystic, same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_INTRIGUE_A
		picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman

		trigger = { FROM = { trait = elusive_shadow religion = ROOT } }
	}
	#Learning mystic, same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_LEARNING_A
		trigger = { FROM = { is_adult = no religion = ROOT } }
	}
	#Diplomacy mystic, different religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_DIPLOMACY_B
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = grey_eminence NOT = { religion = ROOT } } }
	}
	#Martial mystic, different religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_MARTIAL_B
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = barbarian_4 NOT = { religion = ROOT } } }
	}
	#Intrigue mystic, different religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_INTRIGUE_B
		picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman

		trigger = { FROM = { trait = elusive_shadow NOT = { religion = ROOT } } }
	}
	#Learning mystic, different religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal20_LEARNING_B
		trigger = { FROM = { is_adult = no NOT = { religion = ROOT } } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Stay for same religion
	option = {
		trigger = { religion = FROM }
		name = EVTOPTeternal20_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = consorts_with_mystics years = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#The quest is started
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.21 } }

			#Someone becomes concerned
			character_event = { id = eternal.500 days = 60 }
		}

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_sought_mystic_knowledge }
		}
	}

	#Leave for same religion
	option = {
		trigger = { religion = FROM }
		name = EVTOPTeternal20_B
		piety = 50
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}

	#Stay for different religion
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = FROM } }
		name = EVTOPTeternal20_C

		piety = -100

		add_character_modifier = { name = consorts_with_infidels years = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			#The quest is started
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.21 } }

			#Someone becomes concerned
			character_event = { id = eternal.500 days = 60 }
		}

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_sought_mystic_knowledge }
		}

		if = { limit = { trait = zealous } remove_trait = zealous }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { religion_group = old_world_group }
				NOT = { trait = sympathy_old_world_group FROM = { religion_group = ROOT } }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
				NOT = { trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group FROM = { religion_group = ROOT } }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_ormazd_gods_group
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { religion_group = oriental_gods_group }
				NOT = { trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group FROM = { religion_group = ROOT } }
			}
			add_trait = sympathy_oriental_gods_group
		}
	}
	#Leave for different religion
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = FROM } }
		name = EVTOPTeternal20_D
		piety = 100
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
	#Imprison for different religion
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = FROM } }
		name = EVTOPTeternal20_E
		piety = 100

		FROM = { imprison = yes }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.509 days = 60 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.21 The quest starts
# Hidden. The mystic sets the first trial of her path and may start an
# investigation (eternal.500).
# Scope: ROOT is the mystic, FROM the ruler. Fired by eternal.20.
narrative_event = {
	id = eternal.21
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { trait = grey_eminence }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.101 days = 150 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = barbarian_4 }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.201 days = 150 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = elusive_shadow }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.301 days = 150 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_adult = no }
			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.401 days = 150 } }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.101 The Fellowship of the Glade
# Norscan seeress (diplomacy) trial 1: A feast to win a fellowship of
# warriors. Takes the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal101
	id = eternal.101
	desc = EVTDESCeternal101
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_feast

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.101 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal101_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal101_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = -1

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.102 days = 60 } }
				}
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 22 }
					#success
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.104 days = 60 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal101_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal101_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.102 days = 10 } }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.104 days = 10 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal101_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal101_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.111 An Obstacle in Your Path

	title = EVTNAMEeternal111
	id = eternal.111
	desc = EVTDESCeternal111
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_diplomacy

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		text = EVTDESCeternal111
		picture = GFX_evt_rider_adventure
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } }
		text = EVTDESCeternal111
		picture = GFX_evt_pagan
	}

		is_liege_of = FROM

			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...

		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal111_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					#fail
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.112 days = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
				}
				35 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.114 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal111_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal111_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				35 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.112 days = 3 } }
				}
				15 = {
					#horrible failure
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.117 days = 3 }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 diplomacy = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.114 days = 3 } }

			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }

# eternal.123 A Most Convincing Offer
# Norscan seeress (diplomacy) trial 3 passed. The ruler becomes immortal.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal123
	id = eternal.123
	desc = EVTDESCeternal123
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_garden

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal123_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal123_A_TOOLTIP }

		add_trait = immortal
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}

		if = { limit = { trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { add_trait = immortal }

			FROM = { remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_2 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_3 }
		}
	}
}

# The Araby marid-shaman (eternal.201-228)

# eternal.201 In Honor of the Ancestors
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 1: A sacrifice to the ancestors. Takes
# the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal201
	id = eternal.201
	desc = EVTDESCeternal201
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}
	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.201 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	#Choose to sacrifice a antelope
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal201_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal201_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = -1

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.202 days = 40 random = 5 } }
				}
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					#success
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.204 days = 40 random = 5 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Choose to sacrifice a barrel of grain
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal201_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal201_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.202 days = 30 random = 2 } }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.204 days = 30 random = 2 } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_grain
		}
	}
	#Freak out over death omen
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal201_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal201_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.203 A Bloody Mishap
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 1 failed. The mystic leaves court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal203
	id = eternal.203
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.203 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal203_A
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_grain } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal203_B
		trigger = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_grain }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal203_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal203_A_TOOLTIP }

		if = { limit = { trait = one_handed } add_trait = mangled }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = one_handed } } add_trait = one_handed }

		add_trait = severely_injured

		resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes

		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_grain
			FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes }
		}
	}
}
