# eternal.211 [From.GetFirstName]'s Sacred Jar
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 2: The serpent in the sacred jar. Takes
# the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal211
	id = eternal.211
	desc = EVTDESCeternal211
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.211 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	#wring its neck
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal211_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal211_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					#horrible failure
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.217 days = 3 }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.214 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#smash it with a book
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal211_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal211_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					#fail
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.212 days = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
				}
				35 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.214 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_defense
		}
	}
	#run away
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal211_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal211_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal215_A
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_chose_defense } }

			FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.216 days = 300 random = 300 } }

		}
		FROM = {

		}
	}

# eternal.221 A Trial of Faith
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 3: The spirit draught. Takes the safe,
# the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal221
	id = eternal.221
	desc = EVTDESCeternal221
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_ritual_scroll

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.221 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal221_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal221_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					#failure and reincarnation death
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }

					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
						create_character = {
							female = yes
							age = 0
							random_traits = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							religion = ROOT
							culture = ROOT
							race = ROOT
						}
						new_character = { set_mother = ROOT }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { is_female = no } }
						create_character = {
							female = no
							age = 0
							random_traits = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							religion = ROOT
							culture = ROOT
							race = ROOT
						}
						new_character = { set_father = ROOT }
					}

					new_character = {
						# Set Racial Trait
						character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
						narrative_event = { id = eternal.600 days = 3 } #Copies traits
						narrative_event = { id = eternal.226 days = 3 } #Pings new character
					}
				}
				40 = {
					#success ghost
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.222 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal221_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal221_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					#failure and madness
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.228 days = 3 }
				}
				25 = {
					#success search
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 martial = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.224 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal221_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal221_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.225 Immortal Ancestors
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 3 passed. The ruler becomes immortal.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal225
	id = eternal.225
	desc = EVTDESCeternal225
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_shadowy_cabal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal225_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal225_A_TOOLTIP }

		add_trait = immortal
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}

		if = { limit = { trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { add_trait = immortal }
			FROM = { remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_2 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_3 }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.226 Trial 3 reincarnation ping
# Hidden. Tells the newborn the ruler's soul has moved in (eternal.600 copies
# the traits).
# Scope: ROOT is the mystic or the newborn, FROM the ruler. Fired by the trial
# 3 challenge.
narrative_event = {
	id = eternal.226
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.227 } }
	}
}

# eternal.227 The Final Ritual
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 3: the soul leaves the old body and moves
# into a newborn of the court. The ruler dies.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal227
	id = eternal.227
	desc = EVTDESCeternal227
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal227_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal227_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { leave_court_effect = yes }
			death = {
				death_reason = death_murder_unknown
				killer = event_target:eternal_life_mystic
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.228 An Open Spirit Door
# Araby marid-shaman (martial) trial 3 fails and an angry spirit possesses the
# ruler. The mystic leaves.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal228
	id = eternal.228
	desc = EVTDESCeternal228
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal228_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal228_A_TOOLTIP }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = possessed } } add_trait = possessed }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.301 Trial of the Body
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 1: Pick a pocket in the market. Takes
# the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal301
	id = eternal.301
	desc = EVTDESCeternal301
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.301 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal301_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal301_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = -1

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				40 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.302 days = 65 } }
				}
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					#success
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.304 days = 65 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal301_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal301_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.302 days = 65 } }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.304 days = 65 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal301_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal301_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.302 Trial 1 fail ping

# eternal.303 An Encounter with [Root.Religion.GetHighGodName]
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 1 failed. The mystic leaves court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal303
	id = eternal.303
	desc = EVTDESCeternal303
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_market

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.303 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal303_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal303_A_TOOLTIP }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = one_handed } } add_trait = one_handed }

		if = { limit = { trait = one_handed } add_trait = mangled }

		add_trait = severely_injured

		resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes

		add_trait = honest
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.305 Fickle Fingers
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 1 passed.

	title = EVTNAMEeternal305
	id = eternal.305
	desc = EVTDESCeternal305
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_market

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.305 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal305_A
		if = { limit = { trait = trusting } remove_trait = trusting }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } add_trait = deceitful }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.306 } } }

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_1 }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.311 Trial of the Mind
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 2: Brew the alchemical method. Takes the
# safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal311
	id = eternal.311
	desc = EVTDESCeternal311
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_library

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.311 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal311_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal311_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					#fail
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.312 days = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
				}
				35 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.314 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal311_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal311_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				35 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.312 days = 3 } }
				}
				15 = {
					#horrible failure
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.317 days = 3 }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.314 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal311_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal311_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.315 Sharp Mind and Deft Hands
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 2 passed.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal315
	id = eternal.315
	desc = EVTDESCeternal315
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_performing_experiments

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal315_A

		if = { limit = { trait = dull } remove_trait = dull }
		if = { limit = { trait = imbecile } remove_trait = imbecile }
		if = { limit = { trait = slow } remove_trait = slow }
		if = { limit = { is_smart_trigger = no } add_trait = shrewd }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				narrative_event = { id = eternal.316 }
				remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_1 }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_2 }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.321 Trial of the Spirit
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 3: Find the Philosopher's Stone in the
# tunnels. Takes the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal321
	id = eternal.321
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_pagan

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.321 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		text = EVTDESCeternal321
		picture = GFX_evt_rider_adventure
	}
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } } text = EVTDESCeternal321 }

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal321_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal321_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					#failure and death
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.326 days = 5 }
				}
				40 = {
					#success ghost
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.322 days = 5 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal321_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal321_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					#failure and madness
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.327 days = 5 }
				}
				25 = {
					#success search
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.324 days = 5 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal321_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal321_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.327 Voices in the Dark
# Strigany alchemist (intrigue) trial 3 failed. The mystic leaves court.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal327
	id = eternal.327
	desc = EVTDESCeternal327
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal327_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal327_A_TOOLTIP }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = lunatic_1 } }
			add_trait = lunatic_1
			set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 10 }
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } } add_trait = stressed }
		hidden_tooltip = { event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# The Nehekharan child (eternal.401-428)

# eternal.401 A Fallen Man
# Nehekharan child (learning) trial 1: Pity for a fallen man (Usirian's
# scales). Takes the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal401
	id = eternal.401
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_drunk

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = kind trait = drunkard trait = charitable trait = just } }
			set_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					trait = kind
					trait = drunkard
					trait = charitable
					trait = just
					trait = temperate
					trait = cruel
					trait = arbitrary
					trait = cynical
				}
			}
			random = { chance = 20 set_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard }
		}
	}

	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal401_A
		trigger = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal401_B
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard } }
	}

	#Give money
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal401_A

		scaled_wealth = -1

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard }
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.404 days = 3 } } }
			set_character_flag = eternal_life_charity
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = eternal_life_sorry_for_drunkard } }
			hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.402 days = 3 } } }
			set_character_flag = eternal_life_liar
		}
	}

	#Admit
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal401_B
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.404 days = 3 } } }
	}

	#Curse
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal401_C

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.402 days = 3 } } }
	}

	#Give up
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal401_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal401_D_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.411 Mind Over Body
# Nehekharan child (learning) trial 2: Fasting alone in the wilderness. Takes
# the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal411
	id = eternal.411
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_pagan

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		text = EVTDESCeternal411
		picture = GFX_evt_mysterious_forest
	}
	desc = { trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } } text = EVTDESCeternal411 }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.411 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal411_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal411_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				65 = {
					#fail
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.412 days = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
				}
				35 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.414 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal411_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal411_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				35 = {
					#fail
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.412 days = 3 } }
				}
				15 = {
					#horrible failure
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.417 days = 3 }
				}
				50 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.414 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal411_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal411_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

		if = { limit = { is_dumb_trigger = no } add_trait = dull }

		}

	}

# eternal.421 The Bonds of Mortality
# Nehekharan child (learning) trial 3: Loosing the soul from the body. Takes
# the safe, the risky or the quitting road.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal421
	id = eternal.421
	desc = EVTDESCeternal421
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		FROM = { prisoner = no }
		is_liege_of = FROM
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.421 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal421_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal421_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					#failure and reincarnation death
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }

					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
						create_character = {
							female = yes
							age = 0
							random_traits = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							religion = ROOT
							culture = ROOT
							race = ROOT
						}
						new_character = { set_mother = ROOT }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { ROOT = { is_female = no } }
						create_character = {
							female = no
							age = 0
							random_traits = no
							dynasty = ROOT
							religion = ROOT
							culture = ROOT
							race = ROOT
						}
						new_character = { set_father = ROOT }
					}

					new_character = {
						# Set Racial Trait
						character_event = { id = aiclean.31 }
						narrative_event = { id = eternal.600 } #Copies traits
						narrative_event = { id = eternal.426 days = 3 } #Pings new character
					}
				}

				40 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.422 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal421_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal421_B_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				75 = {
					#failure and possession
					modifier = { factor = 1000 FROM = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.428 days = 3 }
				}
				25 = {
					#success
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 13 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 16 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 19 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 learning = 22 }
					FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.422 days = 3 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal421_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal421_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes } }
	}
}

# eternal.423 A New Beginning
# Nehekharan child (learning) trial 3 passed. The ruler becomes immortal.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by the quest pings.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal423
	id = eternal.423
	desc = EVTDESCeternal423
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal423_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal423_A_TOOLTIP }
		add_trait = immortal
		if = {
			limit = { is_female = no has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_woman_trigger = yes has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
			create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
		}

		if = { limit = { trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { add_trait = immortal }

			FROM = { remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_2 } }
		}
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_finished_mystic_task_3 }
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The investigation (eternal.500-510)
###############################################################################

# eternal.500 The court grows suspicious
# Hidden. Someone close to the ruler may start looking into the mystic
# (eternal.501).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by eternal.21.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.500

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				current_heir = {
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = in_hiding }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }

					OR = {
						NOR = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
						}
						AND = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
							at_location = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}

			current_heir = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.501 } }

			break = yes
		}
		random_dynasty_member = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_offmap_ruler = no
					is_adult = yes
					prisoner = no
					NOT = { trait = in_hiding }
					NOT = { trait = incapable }

					OR = {
						NOR = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
						}
						AND = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
							at_location = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				narrative_event = { id = eternal.501 }
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_adult = no
					prisoner = yes
					trait = in_hiding
					trait = incapable

					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
						}
						NOT = { at_location = ROOT }
					}
				}
				FROM = { NOT = { trait = elusive_shadow } }
			}
			ROOT = { job_spymaster = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.501 } } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_adult = no
					prisoner = yes
					trait = in_hiding
					trait = incapable

					AND = {
						OR = {
							has_character_modifier = in_seclusion
							ROOT = { has_character_modifier = in_seclusion }
						}
						NOT = { at_location = ROOT }
					}
				}
				FROM = { trait = elusive_shadow }
			}
			ROOT = { job_chancellor = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.501 } } }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.501 The investigation starts

	}

# eternal.502 A serious concern
# The investigator doubts the mystic: dismiss it, allow an inquiry, or turn on
# her.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the investigator. Fired by eternal.501.
narrative_event = {
	title = EVTNAMEeternal502
	id = eternal.502
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble

	#Relative warns about mystic from same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal502_A
		trigger = {
			FROM = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { religion = ROOT }
		}
	}

	#Another person warns about mystic from same religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal502_B
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT } }
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { religion = ROOT }
		}
	}

	#Relative warns about mystic from other religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal502_C
		trigger = {
			FROM = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { NOT = { religion = ROOT } }
		}
	}

	#Another person warns about mystic from other religion
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCeternal502_D
		trigger = {
			FROM = { NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT } }
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { NOT = { religion = ROOT } }
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Nah it's fine
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
		name = EVTOPTeternal502_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal502_A_TOOLTIP }
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice who = ROOT }
		}
	}
	#investigate
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal502_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal502_B_TOOLTIP }
		FROM = { show_scope_change = no opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT } }

		if = { limit = { trait = trusting } remove_trait = trusting }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#Discovers that mystic is fraud
				50 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						event_target:eternal_life_mystic = {
							NOT = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud }
						}
					}

					#Nice characters tell the truth
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = { NOR = { trait = deceitful trait = ambitious trait = envious } }
						}
						FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.503 } }
					}

					#Bad characters lie
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = { OR = { trait = deceitful trait = ambitious trait = envious } }
						}
						FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.505 } }
					}
				}
				#No proof
				30 = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = { NOR = { trait = deceitful trait = ambitious trait = envious } }
						}
						FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.505 } }
					}

					#Bad characters lie
					if = {
						limit = {
							FROM = { OR = { trait = deceitful trait = ambitious trait = envious } }
						}
						FROM = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.503 } }
					}
				}
				#Mystic discovers you
				20 = {
					event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.507 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	#Imprison
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = trusting } }
		name = EVTOPTeternal502_C

		FROM = { show_scope_change = no opinion = { modifier = opinion_took_my_advice who = ROOT } }

		event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { imprison = ROOT }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.509 days = 60 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { narrative_event = { id = eternal.507 days = 10 } }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.508 The mystic's ultimatum
# The mystic demands trust: beg forgiveness, let her go, or throw her in the
# dungeon.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM the mystic. Fired by eternal.507.

	title = EVTNAMEeternal508
	id = eternal.508
	desc = EVTDESCeternal508
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_quarrel
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		prisoner = no
	}

	fail_trigger_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { prisoner = yes } #Try again later, in case you're released from prison...
			repeat_event = { id = eternal.508 days = 35 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal508_A

		if = { limit = { trait = paranoid } remove_trait = paranoid }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = trusting } } add_trait = trusting }
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal508_B

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal508_B_TOOLTIP }

		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_did_not_trust_me who = ROOT }
			hidden_tooltip = { leave_court_effect = yes }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal508_C
		event_target:eternal_life_mystic = { imprison = ROOT }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:eternal_life_mystic = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = mystic_is_fraud } }
					narrative_event = { id = eternal.509 days = 60 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# eternal.600 Reincarnation
# Hidden. Copies the old ruler's traits onto the newborn the soul moved into.

		ROOT = {
			#Add traits of reincarnation source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
			copy_random_personality_trait = event_target:eternal_life_reincarnation_source
		}
	}
}

# Rebirth (eternal.700-706)

# eternal.700 Like the grandfather
# Hidden. A parent dying naturally tells the heir they are just like their
# grandfather.
# Scope: ROOT is the dying parent. Fired by the on_death random list.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.700
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		death_reason = death_natural
		current_heir = {
			is_child_of = ROOT
			NOR = {
				trait = reincarnation
				trait = incapable
				prisoner = yes
				is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
			}
		}
		OR = { father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } mother_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = yes } }
				father_even_if_dead = { save_event_target_as = reincarnation_target }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 mother_even_if_dead = { is_alive = yes } }
				mother_even_if_dead = { save_event_target_as = reincarnation_target }
			}
		}
		current_heir = { character_event = { id = eternal.701 days = 10 random = 10 } }
	}

	option = { }
}

# eternal.701 A deathbed remark
# The heir can look into the grandparent's diary.
# Scope: ROOT is the heir, FROM the dead parent. Fired by eternal.700.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.701
	desc = EVTDESCeternal701
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	ai = no

	trigger = {
		OR = { father = { character = FROM } mother = { character = FROM } }
		NOR = {
			trait = reincarnation
			trait = incapable
			prisoner = yes
			is_inaccessible_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	immediate = {
		event_target:reincarnation_target = { ROOT = { set_reincarnation = PREV } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal701_A
		character_event = { days = 20 id = eternal.702 TOOLTIP = EVTOPTeternal701_A_TOOLTIP }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal701_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTeternal701_B_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

# eternal.705 An ancestor reborn
# Hidden. In a faith that holds the soul is born again among kin (Cathay, the
# Old Faith of Albion, the Asrai, the ancestor spirits of Kislev, and the
# Dawi, whose ancestors watch over every clan), a newborn may be a dead
# grandparent reborn.
# Was gated on vanilla faiths, so it never fired.
# Scope: ROOT is the newborn. Fired by the on_birth random list.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.705
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { # faiths that hold the soul is born again among kin
			religion = draconite
			religion = old_faith
			religion = old_faith_reformed
			religion = asrai_cadai
			religion = kislev_gods
			religion_group = dwarf_gods_group
		}
		OR = {
			father = {
				ai = no
				is_reincarnated = no
				father_even_if_dead = {
					OR = { is_alive = no father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } }
				}
				NOT = { any_sibling = { is_reincarnated = yes } }
			}
			mother = {
				ai = no
				is_reincarnated = no
				father_even_if_dead = {
					OR = { is_alive = no father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } }
				}
				NOT = { any_sibling = { is_reincarnated = yes } }
			}
		}
		NOT = { any_sibling = { is_reincarnated = yes } }
	}

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { father = { ai = no } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					father = { father_even_if_dead = { father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } } }
				}
				father = {
					father_even_if_dead = {
						father_even_if_dead = { ROOT = { set_reincarnation = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_reincarnated = no }
				father = { father_even_if_dead = { ROOT = { set_reincarnation = PREV } } }
			}

			father = { character_event = { id = eternal.706 days = 30 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { mother = { ai = no } is_reincarnated = no }
			if = {
				limit = {
					mother = { father_even_if_dead = { father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } } }
				}
				mother = {
					father_even_if_dead = {
						father_even_if_dead = { ROOT = { set_reincarnation = PREV } }
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_reincarnated = no }
				mother = { father_even_if_dead = { ROOT = { set_reincarnation = PREV } } }
			}

			mother = { character_event = { id = eternal.706 days = 30 } }
		}
	}
}

# eternal.706 The ancestor's eyes
# The parent sees the dead ancestor in the child: believe it or not.
# Scope: ROOT is the parent, FROM the child. Fired by eternal.705.
character_event = {
	id = eternal.706
	desc = { trigger = { religion_group = dwarf_gods_group } text = EVTDESCeternal706_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { religion = kislev_gods } text = EVTDESCeternal706_kislev }
	desc = EVTDESCeternal706
	picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon

	ai = no

	trigger = {
		NOT = { block_general_event_trigger = yes }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal706_A
		piety = 100
		FROM = { add_trait = reincarnation }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTeternal706_B
		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = zealous trait = cynical } } add_trait = cynical }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 3: Mourning (namespace mourning)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: the lovers, friends and rivals of the dead.
#
#   mourning.1        Hidden starter (on_death random list)
#   mourning.2-5      A lover: grief, the tomb, the widow and the memento
#   mourning.6-10     A friend: grief, drink, a new friend
#   mourning.11-15    A rival: gloating, and the stolen skull
#   mourning.16-20    The end of mourning: faith, a cat, a child
#   mourning.21-27    The dead's child and the friend who promised
#   mourning.30-31    A loved wife: the widower's grief (on_death)
#   mourning.32-34    A good heir: the father's despair (on_death)
#   mourning.35-37    A suspicious death: who did it? (on_death random)
#   mourning.38-39    A gifted councillor: the funeral (on_death random)
#   mourning.60       Executioner nicknames (on_death)
#
# Was RIP.29000-29026 (mourning.N-28999) and RIP.4400 in
# rip_flavor_events.txt; mourning.30-39 were 24500-24510 and 4140 in
# mourning_events.txt. Triggers: WHG_death_triggers.txt.
###############################################################################

namespace = mourning

###############################################################################
# Executioner nicknames (mourning.60)
###############################################################################

# mourning.60 Executioner nicknames
# Hidden. A ruler who has executed enough people may earn a grim nickname.
# Scope: ROOT is the executed, killer the executioner. Fired by on_death.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.60
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		death_execution_trigger = yes
		killer = { always = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		killer = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_nickname = no is_adult = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } }
				random_list = {
					5 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_butcher
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOR = { trait = cruel trait = wroth trait = impaler }
						}
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_impaler
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = { ROOT = { death_reason = death_execution_impaling } }
						}
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_flayer
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = { ROOT = { death_reason = death_execution_flaying } }
						}
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_cleansing_flame
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NAND = {
								NOT = { religion = ROOT }
								trait = zealous
								piety = 100
								ROOT = { death_reason = death_execution_burning }
							}
						}
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_witch_hunter
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NAND = {
								OR = {
									NOT = { religion = ROOT }
									ROOT = { has_character_modifier = modifier_witch }
									ROOT = { has_character_modifier = suspected_witch }
									ROOT = { has_character_modifier = known_witch }
									ROOT = { has_character_flag = bg_accused_witch }
									ROOT = { has_character_flag = physician_witch }
								}
								trait = zealous
								piety = 100
								ROOT = {
									is_woman_trigger = yes
									OR = {
										death_reason = death_execution_burning
										death_reason = death_execution_drowning_pit
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_vengeful
						modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { is_rival = ROOT } }
					}
					50 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_betrayer
						modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { is_friend = ROOT } }
					}
					500 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_kingslayer
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NAND = {
								is_nomadic = no
								liege = {
									is_nomadic = no
									character = ROOT
									OR = { tier = KING tier = EMPEROR }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					15 = {
						give_nickname = nick_son_of_evilgod
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOR = { NOT = { piety = -50 } trait = possessed trait = kinslayer }
						}
					}
					4 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_tyrant
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_vassal = {
									has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_tyrant }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					4 = {
						give_nickname = nick_the_oppressor
						modifier = {
							factor = 0
							NOT = {
								any_vassal = {
									has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_tyrant }
								}
							}
						}
					}
					100 = { }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Grief (mourning.1-27)
###############################################################################

# mourning.1 Someone mourns
# Hidden. When someone dies, a lover, friend, rival or a friend with a child
# left behind may react (2, 6, 11, 21).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead. Fired by the on_death random list.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.1

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_lover = { is_alive = yes }
			any_friend = { is_alive = yes }
			any_rival = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		NOT = { death_reason = death_missing }
	}

	option = {
		save_event_target_as = rip_dead

		spouse = { save_event_target_as = rip_widow }

		random_child = { limit = { is_adult = yes } save_event_target_as = rip_child }

		#Lover event
		random_lover = {
			limit = {
				block_general_event_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = grief_timer }
			}
			character_event = { id = mourning.2 days = 20 }
		}

		#Friend event
		random_friend = {
			limit = {
				block_general_event_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = grief_timer }
			}
			save_event_target_as = rip_friend_1

			ROOT = {
				random_friend = {
					limit = {
						block_general_event_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:rip_friend_1
							character = event_target:rip_friend_1
							is_friend = event_target:rip_friend_1
							is_lover = event_target:rip_friend_1
							is_close_relative = event_target:rip_friend_1
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = rip_friend_2
				}
			}

			random_list = {
				75 = { character_event = { id = mourning.6 days = 30 } }
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						ROOT = {
							NOT = {
								any_child = {
									age = 12
									NOR = {
										character = event_target:rip_friend_1
										is_rival = event_target:rip_friend_1
										is_friend = event_target:rip_friend_1
										is_lover = event_target:rip_friend_1
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = mourning.21 days = 30 }
				}
			}
		}

		#Rival event
		random_rival = {
			limit = {
				block_general_event_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = grief_timer }
			}
			save_event_target_as = rip_enemy_1

			liege = {
				any_courtier = {
					limit = {
						block_general_event_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							character = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_friend = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_lover = event_target:rip_enemy_1
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = rip_new_enemy
				}
			}

			ROOT = {
				random_rival = {
					limit = {
						block_general_event_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							character = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_friend = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_lover = event_target:rip_enemy_1
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = rip_enemy_2
				}
				random_child = {
					limit = {
						block_general_event_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							character = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_friend = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_lover = event_target:rip_enemy_1
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = rip_new_enemy
				}
				random_friend = {
					limit = {
						block_general_event_trigger = no
						NOR = {
							is_rival = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							character = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_friend = event_target:rip_enemy_1
							is_lover = event_target:rip_enemy_1
						}
					}
					save_event_target_as = rip_new_enemy
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = mourning.11 days = 40 }
		}
	}
}

# mourning.2 A lover lost
# A lover grieves.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.1.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.2
	desc = EVTDESCmourning2
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = grief_timer years = 10 hidden = yes }
	}

	#Visit their tomb
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning2_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_A_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:rip_widow = { always = yes }
				NOT = { event_target:rip_widow = { character = ROOT } }
			}
			hidden_tooltip = {
				event_target:rip_widow = { character_event = { id = mourning.3 days = 7 } }
			}
			break = yes
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mourning.5 days = 7 } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}

	#Stay in room and cry
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = cynical trait = depressed } }
		name = EVTOPTmourning2_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_B_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }

			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
					}

					#Recover from depression
					random_list = {
						10 = { character_event = { id = mourning.16 days = 200 random = 600 } }
						20 = {
							modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_modifier = pet_cat }
							character_event = { id = mourning.17 days = 200 random = 600 }
						}
						10 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 0
								NOT = { any_child = { age = 3 NOT = { age = 12 } } }
							}
							character_event = { id = mourning.18 days = 200 random = 600 }
						}
						20 = { } #Nothing
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { trait = depressed }
		name = EVTOPTmourning2_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_D_TOOLTIP }

		death = { death_reason = death_suicide_hanging }

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	#Face the world. You're a badass!
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = craven } }

		name = EVTOPTmourning2_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_C_TOOLTIP }

		random_list = {
			33 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cynical }
				add_trait = cynical
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.258 } }
			}
			33 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cruel }
				add_trait = cruel
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.259 } }
			}
			33 = {
				#Nothing
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}
}

# mourning.3 At the tomb
# The widow or widower sits by the grave and may meet the secret lover there.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.2.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.3
	desc = EVTDESCmourning3
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Let it mourn
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = envious trait = wroth } }
		name = EVTOPTmourning3_A

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }

		FROM = { hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mourning.5 days = 3 } } }
	}

	#Lash out
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = kind } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 3 }

		name = EVTOPTmourning3_B

		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_rival = FROM } } add_rival = FROM }

		FROM = { hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mourning.4 days = 3 } } }

		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = envious } } add_trait = envious }
	}
}

# mourning.6 A friend lost
# A friend grieves.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.1.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.6
	desc = EVTDESCmourning6
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = grief_timer years = 10 hidden = yes }
	}

	#Go out drinking
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning6_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning6_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:rip_friend_2 = { always = yes } }
				event_target:rip_friend_2 = { character_event = { id = mourning.7 days = 3 } }
				break = yes
			}

			random_courtier = {
				limit = { block_general_event_trigger = no NOT = { is_friend = ROOT } ai = yes }
				character_event = { id = mourning.7 days = 3 }
				break = yes
			}

			character_event = { id = mourning.9 days = 3 }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 20 }
	}

	#Be alone
	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = cynical trait = depressed } }
		name = EVTOPTmourning6_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_B_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }

			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = depressed
				hidden_tooltip = {
					character_event = {
						id = traitnote.288 #Notify Depressed
					}

					#Recover from depression
					random_list = {
						10 = { character_event = { id = mourning.16 days = 200 random = 600 } }
						20 = {
							modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_modifier = pet_cat }
							character_event = { id = mourning.17 days = 200 random = 600 }
						}
						10 = {
							modifier = {
								factor = 0
								NOT = { any_child = { age = 3 NOT = { age = 12 } } }
							}
							character_event = { id = mourning.18 days = 200 random = 600 }
						}
						20 = { } #Nothing
					}
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}

	#Suicide
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = depressed }
		name = EVTOPTmourning2_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning2_D_TOOLTIP }

		death = { death_reason = death_suicide_hanging }

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}

	#Get rid of all of your friends
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning6_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning6_D_TOOLTIP }
		trigger = { any_friend = { is_alive = yes } }
		any_friend = { remove_friend = ROOT }

		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
	}
}

# mourning.8 Drinking to the dead
# Two mourners drink together and may become friends.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.7.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.8
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_brothel

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = { trigger = { event_target:rip_friend_2 = { always = yes } } text = EVTDESCmourning8_A }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:rip_friend_2 = { always = yes } } }
		text = EVTDESCmourning8_B
	}

	#Let's be friends
	option = {
		name = {
			trigger = { event_target:rip_friend_2 = { always = yes } }
			text = EVTOPTmourning8_A_A
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { NOT = { event_target:rip_friend_2 = { always = yes } } }
			text = EVTOPTmourning8_A_B
		}

		add_friend = FROM

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.10 days = 3 } } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 3 }
	}

	#I don't want your company!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning8_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning8_B_TOOLTIP }

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_rejected_friend who = FROM years = 3 }

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mourning.9 days = 3 } }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.10 days = 3 } } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 }
	}
}

# mourning.11 A rival dies
# Gloat over a dead rival, and perhaps steal their skull.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.1.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.11
	desc = EVTDESCmourning11
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = grief_timer years = 10 hidden = yes }
	}

	#Steal skull
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning11_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning11_A_TOOLTIP }

		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = cruel
				trait = wroth
				trait = impaler
				trait = lunatic_1
				trait = lunatic_2
				trait = lunatic_3
				trait = possessed
				trait = arbitrary
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				#Fail
				40 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							NOT = { event_target:rip_widow = { always = yes } }
							NOT = { event_target:rip_child = { always = yes } }
							event_target:rip_widow = { character = ROOT }
							event_target:rip_child = { character = ROOT }
						}
					}

					if = {
						limit = { event_target:rip_widow = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
						event_target:rip_widow = { character_event = { id = mourning.12 days = 7 } }
						break = yes
					}
					if = {
						limit = { event_target:rip_child = { NOT = { character = ROOT } } }
						event_target:rip_child = { character_event = { id = mourning.12 days = 7 } }
					}
				}
				#Success
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 9 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 12 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 18 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.2 intrigue = 21 }

					character_event = { id = mourning.14 days = 7 }
				}
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 2 }
	}

	#Enemy of my enemy
	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:rip_enemy_2 = { is_alive = yes } }
		name = EVTOPTmourning11_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning11_B_TOOLTIP }

		add_friend = event_target:rip_enemy_2

		ai_chance = { factor = 10 }
	}

	#Get a new enemy
	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:rip_new_enemy = { is_alive = yes } }
		name = EVTOPTmourning11_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning11_C_TOOLTIP }

		add_rival = event_target:rip_new_enemy

		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
	}
	#Repent
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				any_rival = { is_alive = yes }
				trait = cruel
				trait = envious
				NOT = { trait = kind }
			}
		}

		name = EVTOPTmourning11_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning11_D_TOOLTIP }

		if = { limit = { NOR = { trait = cruel trait = envious trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		if = { limit = { trait = envious NOT = { trait = cruel } } remove_trait = envious }
		if = { limit = { trait = cruel } remove_trait = cruel }

		random_rival = { remove_rival = ROOT }
		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }
	}

	#No time
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning11_E
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning11_E_TOOLTIP }

		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }

		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
	}
}

# mourning.12 Caught at the grave
# The rival's spouse or child catches the skull thief.
# Scope: ROOT is the spouse or child, FROM the thief. Fired by mourning.11.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.12
	desc = EVTDESCmourning12
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Imprison
	option = {
		trigger = { is_ruler = yes }

		name = EVTOPTmourning12_A

		#adds rival
		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_rival = FROM } } add_rival = FROM }

		#If they are your liege, you become a traitor
		if = {
			limit = { is_liege_or_above = FROM }

			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_traitor who = FROM years = 20 }
		}

		#If you are their liege, they become a traitor
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_traitor who = FROM years = 20 }
		}

		FROM = { imprison = ROOT }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { set_character_flag = rip_imprisoned character_event = { id = mourning.13 } }
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 3 trait = wroth } }
	}

	#Get out
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning12_B

		if = { limit = { NOT = { is_rival = FROM } } add_rival = FROM }

		#If you are their liege, they become a traitor
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_traitor who = FROM years = 20 }
		}

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning12_B_TOOLTIP }

		ai_chance = { factor = 5 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.13 } } }
	}
}

# mourning.13 Caught with the head
# The thief is caught in the act.
# Scope: ROOT is the thief. Fired by mourning.12.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.13
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools

	is_triggered_only = yes

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = rip_imprisoned }
		text = EVTDESCmourning13_A
		picture = GFX_evt_into_the_dungeon
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = rip_imprisoned } }
		text = EVTDESCmourning13_B
		picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning13_A

		add_rival = FROM

		#If they are your liege, you become a traitor
		if = {
			limit = { is_liege_or_above = FROM }
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_traitor who = FROM years = 20 }
		}

		add_character_modifier = { name = graverobber years = 10 }

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = rip_imprisoned }

			imprison = FROM

			#If you are their liege, they become a traitor
			if = {
				limit = { FROM = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_traitor who = FROM years = 20 }
			}

			clr_character_flag = rip_imprisoned
		}
	}
}

# mourning.21 A promise to the dead
# The mourner promised the dead to look after their child.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, rip_dead the dead. Fired by mourning.1.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.21
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	desc = EVTDESCmourning21
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = grief_timer years = 10 hidden = yes }

		event_target:rip_dead = {
			if = {
				limit = { any_child = { ai = no } }

				random_child = {
					limit = { ai = no NOR = { is_rival = ROOT is_friend = ROOT is_lover = ROOT } }
					save_event_target_as = rip_heir
				}
				break = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_child = { is_heir = yes }
					NOR = { is_rival = ROOT is_friend = ROOT is_lover = ROOT }
				}

				random_child = { limit = { is_heir = yes } save_event_target_as = rip_heir }
				break = yes
			}
			random_child = {
				limit = { age = 12 NOR = { is_rival = ROOT is_friend = ROOT is_lover = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = rip_heir
			}
		}
	}

	#Approach
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning21_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning21_A_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:rip_heir = { character_event = { id = mourning.22 days = 7 } }
		}
		event_target:rip_heir = { set_character_flag = rip_flag clr_character_flag = rip_flag }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 7
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = gregarious trait = kind trait = just } }
		}
	}

	#Or become lovers...?
	option = {
		trigger = {
			#Must be appropriate love target
			event_target:rip_heir = {
				is_adult = yes
				NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				root_attraction_trigger = yes
			}

			#Root must be motivated
			OR = {
				event_target:rip_heir = { trait = fair }
				trait = lustful
				trait = seducer
				trait = seductress
				AND = { age = 40 has_lover = no is_married = no }
			}

			#Root can't be unmotivated
			NOR = { trait = chaste trait = celibate }
		}
		name = EVTOPTmourning21_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning21_C_TOOLTIP }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:rip_heir = { character_event = { id = mourning.25 days = 7 } }
		}

		event_target:rip_heir = { set_character_flag = rip_flag clr_character_flag = rip_flag }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = lustful
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.272 } } #Notify Lustful
			}
		}

		ai_chance = { factor = 15 }
	}

	#Don't approach
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning21_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmourning21_B_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = shy trait = gregarious } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = shy
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.263 } } #Notify Shy
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = gregarious }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				remove_trait = gregarious
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = traitnote.297 } } #Notify remove gregarious
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = shy trait = cruel trait = arbitrary } }
		}
	}
}

# mourning.22 An offer of friendship

		add_friend = FROM
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.23 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 7
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = gregarious trait = kind trait = just } }
		}
	}

	#I am fine on my own
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning22_B

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_rejected_friend years = 5 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.24 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = { factor = 2 OR = { trait = shy trait = cruel trait = arbitrary } }
		}
	}
}

# mourning.25 The dead parent's lover
# The dead parent's lover offers to look after the orphan.
# Scope: ROOT is the orphan, FROM the lover. Fired by mourning.21.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.25
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	desc = EVTDESCmourning25
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Let's be lovers
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning25_A

		if = {
			limit = { is_no_females_race = no FROM = { is_no_females_race = no } }
			add_lover = FROM
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.26 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = { factor = 5 OR = { trait = lustful trait = seducer trait = seductress } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = { FROM = { root_attraction_trigger = no } trait = celibate }
			}
		}
	}

	#No!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmourning25_B

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_advance_rejected years = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = mourning.27 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 3
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = { trait = shy trait = chaste is_ascetic_trigger = yes is_married = yes }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 5 has_lover = yes }
		}
	}
}

# A wife, an heir, a friend, a councillor (mourning.30-39)
# Was 24500-24510 and 4140 in mourning_events.txt.

# mourning.30 A loved wife dies
# Hidden. A ruler's wife dies, and her husband loved her well and is kind
# or charitable. In two months he decides how to live with it (31).
# Fixed: the dying wife was shown an event of her own.
# Scope: ROOT is the dead wife. Fired by on_death.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.30
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	is_married = yes

	trigger = {
		is_woman_trigger = yes
		immortal = no
		spouse = { mourning_loving_widower_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		spouse = { character_event = { id = mourning.31 days = 64 } }
	}
}

# mourning.31 The empty bed
# Seek comfort in other arms (lustful, or 1 in 10 the grief unmans him:
# incapable), or give the rest of his life to grief (depressed; a man of the
# Old World faiths swears before Morr never to take another, celibate).
# Scope: ROOT is the widower, FROM the dead wife.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.31
	desc = EVTDESCmourning31
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes # mourning.30

	option = { # Other arms
		name = EVTOPTAmourning31
		random_list = {
			90 = { if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = lustful } } add_trait = lustful } }
			10 = { add_trait = incapable }
		}
	}
	option = { # Grief
		name = EVTOPTBmourning31
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } } add_trait = depressed }
		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = old_world_group NOT = { trait = celibate } }
			add_trait = celibate
		}
	}
}

# mourning.32 The good heir dies
# Hidden. A capable heir (martial and stewardship 8) dies, and his weaker
# brother is next. Four months later the father faces it (33).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead heir. Fired by on_death.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.32
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death
	only_men = yes
	min_age = 15

	trigger = {
		is_primary_heir = yes
		martial = 8
		stewardship = 8
		father = { is_alive = yes is_ruler = yes NOT = { trait = stressed } }
		any_sibling = { is_adult = yes is_female = no NOT = { martial = 8 } }
	}

	immediate = {
		father = { character_event = { id = mourning.33 days = 128 } }
	}
}

# mourning.33 A father's despair
# The heir is gone and the next son is no soldier. Get another heir on a
# young wife (34), or make the boy a man (9 in 10 it breaks him: craven;
# 1 in 10 brave).
# Scope: ROOT is the father, FROM the dead heir.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.33
	desc = EVTDESCmourning33
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes # mourning.32

	option = { # Another son
		name = EVTOPTAmourning33
		trigger = {
			is_married = yes
			spouse = { is_woman_trigger = yes is_pregnant = no fertility = 0.4 NOT = { age = 30 } }
		}
		character_event = { id = mourning.34 days = 2 }
	}
	option = { # Make a man of the boy
		name = EVTOPTBmourning33
		random_child = {
			limit = { is_adult = yes is_female = no is_alive = yes NOT = { martial = 8 } }
			random_list = {
				90 = {
					if = {
						limit = { NOR = { trait = berserker trait = craven } }
						add_trait = craven
					}
				}
				10 = { if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = brave } } add_trait = brave } }
			}
		}
	}
}

	}
