# mourning.35 A suspicious death
# Hidden. A young person (15 to 34) is murdered by an unknown hand, and a
# courtier who loved them suspects someone (36). A ward killed by a
# guardian (WHG_guardian_events) is mourned by the father, or the mother
# if he is dead, and the guardian is the suspect.
# Fixed: a guardian's murder looked for the guardian among the dead child's
# own courtiers, so no one was ever suspected; each option of 36 picked a
# different random suspect. The true killer is now saved, and a courtier
# killer is the likeliest suspect. The suspect is sought in the whole court
# (the old event searched the mourner's own courtiers, which a courtier
# does not have).
# Scope: ROOT is the dead. Fired by the on_death random list.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.35
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death random_events

	trigger = {
		death_murder_unknown_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_character_flag = was_murdered_by_plot }
		OR = {
			has_character_flag = murdered_by_guardian
			AND = {
				age = 15
				NOT = { age = 35 }
				any_courtier = { mourning_grieving_friend_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		killer = { save_event_target_as = true_killer }
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = murdered_by_guardian }
			clr_character_flag = murdered_by_guardian
			if = {
				limit = { father = { is_alive = yes } }
				father = {
					set_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child
					character_event = { id = mourning.36 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { mother = { is_alive = yes } }
				mother = {
					set_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child
					character_event = { id = mourning.36 }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { mourning_grieving_friend_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = mourning.36 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# mourning.36 Who did this?
# Keep a grudge against the suspect, have them jailed (a ruler), or take it
# to the liege (37). A courtier with intrigue 8 is suspected, the true
# killer most likely if they are at court. A parent suspects the guardian.
# Scope: ROOT is the mourner, FROM the dead.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.36
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child } text = EVTDESCmourning36b }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child } }
		text = EVTDESCmourning36
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes # mourning.35

	trigger = {
		is_inaccessible_trigger = no
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child }
			event_target:true_killer = { save_event_target_as = murder_suspect }
		}
		else = {
			host = {
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						NOT = { character = ROOT }
						NOT = { character = FROM }
						prisoner = no
						age = 16
						intrigue = 8
						NOT = { trait = incapable }
					}
					preferred_limit = { character = event_target:true_killer }
					save_event_target_as = murder_suspect
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # A grudge
		name = EVTOPTAmourning36
		trigger = { event_target:murder_suspect = { always = yes } }
		event_target:murder_suspect = {
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = suspect_of_murder who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
	}
	option = { # Jail them
		name = EVTOPTBmourning36
		trigger = {
			is_ruler = yes
			event_target:murder_suspect = {
				OR = { host = { character = ROOT } liege = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		event_target:murder_suspect = { imprison = ROOT }
	}
	option = { # Tell my lord
		name = EVTOPTCmourning36
		trigger = { is_ruler = no event_target:murder_suspect = { always = yes } }
		event_target:murder_suspect = {
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = suspect_of_murder who = ROOT years = 10 }
		}
		liege = { character_event = { id = mourning.37 } }
	}
	option = { # Let it go
		name = EVTOPTDmourning36
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:murder_suspect = { always = yes } } }
	}

	after = {
		clr_character_flag = guardian_murdered_child
	}
}

# mourning.37 An accusation
# A courtier names a murderer. Ignore it (they feel betrayed) or jail the
# suspect, if they are the liege's to jail (they are satisfied).
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the accuser.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.37
	desc = EVTDESCmourning37
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes # mourning.36

	option = { # Ignore it
		name = EVTOPTAmourning37
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_betrayed who = ROOT years = 5 } }
	}
	option = { # Jail them
		name = EVTOPTBmourning37
		trigger = {
			event_target:murder_suspect = {
				prisoner = no
				OR = { host = { character = ROOT } liege = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		event_target:murder_suspect = { imprison = ROOT }
		FROM = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_satisfied who = ROOT years = 2 } }
	}
}

# mourning.38 A councillor dies young
# Hidden. A gifted councillor (a skill of 15) under 50 dies serving a
# count or duke who did not kill them; the liege leads the funeral (39).
# Fixed: the old check that the liege was not the killer failed for every
# death without a killer, so only murders ever led to a funeral.
# Scope: ROOT is the dead. Fired by the on_death random list.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.38
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes # on_death random_events

	trigger = {
		is_councillor = yes
		independent = no
		NOT = { age = 50 }
		OR = { diplomacy = 15 martial = 15 intrigue = 15 learning = 15 stewardship = 15 }
		NOR = { death_no_trace_trigger = yes death_in_cathay_trigger = yes }
		liege = {
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			OR = { tier = DUKE tier = COUNT }
			NOT = { spouse = { character = ROOT } }
			is_inaccessible_trigger = no
			NOT = { ROOT = { killer = { character = PREVPREV } } }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		liege = { character_event = { id = mourning.39 } }
	}
}

# mourning.39 The funeral procession
# The liege walks behind the bier: in true grief (Sincere for five years;
# the text follows the faith), for show (Cynical), or pays for the funeral
# and gives the widow or widower a purse (25 gold; they are grateful).
# Fixed: the purse option asked for 10 gold of debt instead of costing
# anything; the three faith options are one.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the dead councillor.
character_event = {
	id = mourning.39
	desc = EVTDESCmourning39
	picture = GFX_evt_death

	is_triggered_only = yes # mourning.38

	option = { # True grief
		name = { trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group } text = EVTOPTAmourning39 }
		name = { trigger = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } text = EVTOPTAmourning39b }
		name = {
			trigger = {
				NOR = { religion_group = old_world_group religion_group = ormazd_gods_group }
			}
			text = EVTOPTAmourning39c
		}
		add_character_modifier = { name = the_sincere years = 5 }
	}
	option = { # For show
		name = EVTOPTBmourning39
		add_character_modifier = { name = the_cynical years = 5 }
	}
	option = { # A purse for the family
		name = EVTOPTCmourning39
		trigger = { wealth = 25 FROM = { spouse = { is_alive = yes } } }
		wealth = -25
		FROM = { spouse = { opinion = { modifier = charitable_pos who = ROOT years = 10 } } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 4: Suicide (namespace suicide)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: weary immortals and the incapable, through the commit_suicide
# decisions (dynasty_decisions, rip_various_decisions).
#
#   suicide.1-10      The ways to end it: a duel with a rival, poison, the
#                     rope, a fall
#   suicide.11-13     An incapable character begs a carer for help
#
# Was RIP.30200-30212 in rip_flavor_events.txt.
###############################################################################

namespace = suicide

# suicide.1 Weary of eternity
# An immortal (or anyone taking the decision) chooses how to end it: a duel
# with a rival, poison, the rope or a fall.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by the commit_suicide decisions
# (dynasty_decisions, rip_various_decisions).
character_event = {
	id = suicide.1
	picture = GFX_evt_depopulated_town
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	hide_from = yes

	desc = { trigger = { immortal = yes } text = EVTDESCsuicide1_A }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			immortal = no
			NOR = {
				any_child = { is_alive = yes }
				any_lover = { is_alive = yes }
				any_friend = { is_alive = yes }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCsuicide1_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			immortal = no
			OR = {
				any_child = { is_alive = yes }
				any_lover = { is_alive = yes }
				any_friend = { is_alive = yes }
			}
		}
		text = EVTDESCsuicide1_C
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = suicide.2 days = 365 } #Cleanup

		random_rival = {
			limit = { block_general_event_trigger = no }
			save_event_target_as = suicide_duel_target
		}
	}

	#Challenge rival to duel
	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:suicide_duel_target = { always = yes } }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide1_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTsuicide1_A_TOOLTIP }

		event_target:suicide_duel_target = {
			hidden_tooltip = { letter_event = { id = suicide.3 days = 1 } }
		}
	}

	#Drink poison
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide1_B

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					set_character_flag = suicide_success
					modifier = { factor = 2 intrigue = 15 }
					modifier = { factor = 10 immortal = no }
				}
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 7 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 8 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 9 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { intrigue = 10 } }
					set_character_flag = suicide_fail
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = suicide.8 days = 14 }
		}
	}

	#Hang yourself
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide1_C

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					modifier = { factor = 10 immortal = no }
					set_character_flag = suicide_success
				}
				70 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						NOT = { any_courtier = { block_general_event_trigger = no ai = yes } }
					}
					set_character_flag = suicide_fail
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = suicide.9 days = 14 }
		}
	}

	#Throw yourself off a cliff
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide1_D

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 10 immortal = no }
					set_character_flag = suicide_success
				}
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 7 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 8 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 9 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 health = 10 }
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_strong_trigger = yes }
					set_character_flag = suicide_fail
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = suicide.10 days = 14 }
		}
	}
}

# suicide.2 Cleanup
# Hidden. Clears the suicide flags.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by suicide.1.
character_event = {
	id = suicide.2
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		clr_character_flag = suicide_fail
		clr_character_flag = suicide_success
		clr_character_flag = lost_suicide_duel
		clr_character_flag = incapable_suicide_duel
		clr_character_flag = won_suicide_duel
		clr_character_flag = attempting_suicide
	}
}

# suicide.3 A challenge
# The rival receives the challenge to a duel.
# Scope: ROOT is the rival, FROM the challenger. Fired by suicide.1.
letter_event = {
	id = suicide.3
	desc = EVTDESCsuicide3
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Yes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide3_A

		FROM = { letter_event = { id = suicide.4 days = 14 } }

		ai_chance = { factor = 100 modifier = { factor = 0.01 trait = craven } }
	}

	#No
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide3_B

		FROM = { letter_event = { id = suicide.5 days = 14 } }

		add_character_modifier = { name = wol_refused_duel years = 10 }

		ai_chance = { factor = 2 }
	}
}

# suicide.4 The challenge accepted
# The challenger hears the rival will fight.
# Scope: ROOT is the challenger, FROM the rival. Fired by suicide.3.
letter_event = {
	id = suicide.4
	desc = EVTDESCsuicide4
	border = GFX_event_letter_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide4_A

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					modifier = { factor = 2 immortal = no }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 1 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 2 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 4 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 5 } }

					set_character_flag = lost_suicide_duel
				}
				20 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 1 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 2 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 3 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 4 } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { combat_rating = 5 } }
					set_character_flag = incapable_suicide_duel
				}
				30 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { NOT = { combat_rating = 0 } } }
					set_character_flag = won_suicide_duel
				}
			}

			character_event = { id = suicide.6 days = 14 }
		}
	}
}

	}
}

# suicide.6 The duel
# The challenger lets the rival win, or the plan fails.
# Scope: ROOT is the challenger. Fired by suicide.4.
character_event = {
	id = suicide.6
	picture = GFX_evt_melee
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_suicide_duel } text = EVTDESCsuicide6_LOST }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = won_suicide_duel } text = EVTDESCsuicide6_WON }
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = incapable_suicide_duel }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide6_INCAPABLE
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#You died in the duel
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = lost_suicide_duel }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide6_A

		prestige = -100

		death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = event_target:suicide_duel_target }

		event_target:suicide_duel_target = {
			tooltip = { prestige = 100 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = suicide.7 } }
		}
	}

	#Rival didn't kill you
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = won_suicide_duel }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide6_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTsuicide5_A_TOOLTIP }

		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:suicide_duel_target = { character_event = { id = suicide.7 } }
		}
	}

	#Rival didn't kill you but you kill them
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = won_suicide_duel
			event_target:suicide_duel_target = { ai = yes }
			NOT = { trait = kind }
		}
		name = EVTOPTsuicide6_C

		prestige = 100

		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes

		random = { chance = 50 add_trait_wroth_effect = yes }

		event_target:suicide_duel_target = {
			prestige = -100

			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
	}

	#You become incapable in the duel
	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = incapable_suicide_duel }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide6_D
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTsuicide5_A_TOOLTIP }

		prestige = -100

		event_target:suicide_duel_target = {
			tooltip = { prestige = 100 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = suicide.7 } }
		}

		add_trait = incapable
	}
}

# suicide.7 A strange duel
# The rival realises the challenger wanted to die.

	}

		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = won_suicide_duel } }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide7_LOST

		FROM = { tooltip = { prestige = -100 death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT } } }
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = incapable_suicide_duel } }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide7_B

		prestige = 100

		FROM = { tooltip = { prestige = -100 add_trait = incapable } }
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { FROM = { has_character_flag = won_suicide_duel } }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide7_C
	}
}

		}

# suicide.11 Asking for help
# An incapable character begs a carer to end it.
# Scope: ROOT is the incapable character, suicide_assistant the carer. Fired
# by the commit_suicide decisions (dynasty_decisions, rip_various_decisions).
character_event = {
	id = suicide.11
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide11_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } } }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide11_B
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_friend = {
			limit = {
				block_general_event_trigger = no
				NOT = { has_character_flag = refused_assisted_suicide }
			}
			save_event_target_as = suicide_assistant
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } } }
			random_lover = {
				limit = {
					block_general_event_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = refused_assisted_suicide }
				}
				save_event_target_as = suicide_assistant
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } } }
			host = {
				random_courtier = {
					limit = {
						has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						NOT = { has_character_flag = refused_assisted_suicide }
					}
					save_event_target_as = suicide_assistant
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } } }
			random_child = {
				limit = {
					block_general_event_trigger = no
					NOT = { has_character_flag = refused_assisted_suicide }
				}
				save_event_target_as = suicide_assistant
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide11_A

		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:suicide_assistant = { character_event = { id = suicide.12 days = 5 } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { event_target:suicide_assistant = { always = yes } } }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide11_B

		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = suicide_success
			character_event = { id = suicide.13 days = 5 }
		}
	}
}

# suicide.12 A terrible request
# The carer decides whether to help.
# Scope: ROOT is the carer, FROM the incapable character. Fired by suicide.11.
character_event = {
	id = suicide.12
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick
	desc = EVTDESCsuicide12
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Yes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide12_A

		FROM = {
			tooltip = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown_strangle killer = ROOT } }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = suicide_success
				character_event = { id = suicide.13 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 33

			modifier = { factor = 2 is_benevolent_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brave }
			modifier = {
				factor = 4
				trait = ambitious
				FROM = { current_heir = { character = ROOT } }
			}
		}
	}

	#No
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTsuicide12_B

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_distressing_encounter years = 5 }
		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_distressing_encounter years = 5 }

		set_character_flag = refused_assisted_suicide

		FROM = {
			hidden_tooltip = {
				set_character_flag = suicide_fail
				character_event = { id = suicide.13 }
			}
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 67

			modifier = { factor = 2 is_evil_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = craven }
		}
	}
}

# suicide.13 The answer
# The incapable character hears the carer's answer.
# Scope: ROOT is the incapable character, FROM the carer. Fired by suicide.11,
# suicide.12.
character_event = {
	id = suicide.13
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = suicide_success NOT = { FROM = { character = ROOT } } }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide13_SUCCESS_A
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = suicide_success FROM = { character = ROOT } }
		text = EVTDESCsuicide13_SUCCESS_B
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = suicide_fail } text = EVTDESCsuicide13_FAIL }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = suicide_success }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide8_A

		death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown_strangle killer = FROM }
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = suicide_fail }
		name = EVTOPTsuicide13_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTsuicide5_A_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 5: Chess with Death (namespace chess)
# Faction: Old World faiths
# Serves: human, dwarf and elf player rulers; once per life.
#
# Death walks the ruler's garden as a pilgrim, reveals itself with a dagger,
# and can be played at chess for a few more years. For humans it comes as a
# grey-robed priest of Morr (a stranger's god to humans of other faiths),
# for the Dawi as a wandering priest of Gazul, for the elves as a servant of
# Morai-Heg. One time in four, for every faith alike, it is a daemon of
# Chaos playing for the soul: lose, and the ruler may give the soul up to live on,
# possessed. Humans, dwarfs and elves only; never the immortal.
#   chess.1-6         The pilgrim of Morr and the reveal
#   chess.7-13        The game
#   chess.14          Death's gift cures the next disease
#
# Was RIP.30220-30233 in rip_flavor_events.txt. The mod had Death appear
# as a daemon of Chaos for every faith outside the Old World; the daemon is
# now a one-in-four opponent for every faith (Komi, 2026-11-07).
###############################################################################

namespace = chess

# chess.1 Death comes walking
# Hidden. Death comes in the guise of a pilgrim: for humans of the Old World
# faiths a grey-robed priest or priestess of Morr, for the Dawi a
# wandering priest of Gazul, for the elves a servant of Morai-Heg. One time in
# four, for every faith alike, it is no servant of death at
# all but a daemon of Chaos, come to play for the ruler's soul. More likely
# in old age, poor health and during the Nurgle plagues or the Red Pox. Once
# per life; never for the immortal, who have nothing left to wager.
# Scope: ROOT is a human, dwarf or elf player ruler. Fired by the
# on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = chess.1
	hide_window = yes

	min_age = 16
	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	war = no
	ai = no

	trigger = {
		OR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_dwarf trait = creature_elf }
		is_nomadic = no
		NOT = { has_character_flag = chess_with_death }
		immortal = no
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			any_realm_province = {
				OR = { disease = nurgles_rot disease = creeping_buboes disease = typhus }
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 age = 70 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.4 NOT = { health = 2 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 NOT = { health = 4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = gamer }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = chess_with_death

		random_list = {
			25 = { #A daemon plays for the soul, the same odds for every faith
				set_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon
			}
			75 = { }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon }
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = yes
						name = Vaingaitha
						religion = chaos
						culture = daemon_chaos
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = undivided_demon
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = no
						name = Czinaic
						religion = chaos
						culture = daemon_chaos
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = undivided_demon
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_dwarf }
			set_character_flag = chess_voice_dawi
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = yes
						religion = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = nun
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_dwarf
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = no
						religion = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = monk
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_dwarf
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { trait = creature_elf }
			set_character_flag = chess_voice_elf
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = yes
						religion = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = nun
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_elf
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = no
						religion = ROOT
						culture = ROOT
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = monk
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_elf
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = yes
						religion = morrian
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = nun
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_human
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
				50 = {
					create_character = {
						random_traits = no
						age = 32
						dynasty = none
						female = no
						religion = morrian
						trait = mastermind_theologian
						trait = monk
						trait = chaste
						trait = shrewd
						trait = gamer
						trait = patient
						trait = fatigue
						trait = on_pilgrimage
						trait = creature_human
						attributes = { martial = 5 }
					}
					new_character = { save_event_target_as = death }
				}
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_walk
		event_target:death = { health = 10 character_event = { id = chess.2 } }
	}
}

# chess.2 Death's turn
# Hidden. Moves the chain on from Death's side.
# Scope: ROOT is Death, FROM the ruler. Fired by chess.1, chess.10, chess.11,
# chess.12, chess.3, chess.4, chess.5, chess.7, chess.8, chess.9.
character_event = {
	id = chess.2
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			trigger_switch = {
				on_trigger = has_character_flag
				chess_walk = { character_event = { id = chess.3 } } #Seeing someone during a walk
				chess_30223 = { character_event = { id = chess.4 days = 5 } } #Talking to the nun/monk, they want room & board
				chess_reveal = { narrative_event = { id = chess.5 days = 14 random = 14 } } #Death comes for you
				chess_30225 = { character_event = { id = chess.6 days = 1 } } #Outcome of attacking death
				chess_30226 = { character_event = { id = chess.7 days = 5 } } #Picking chess pieces
				chess_30227 = { character_event = { id = chess.8 days = 3 } } #First move
				chess_30228 = { character_event = { id = chess.9 days = 1 } } #Outcome of first move
				chess_30229 = { character_event = { id = chess.10 days = 5 } } #Another tactic
				chess_30230 = { character_event = { id = chess.11 days = 1 } } #Outcome of the tactic
				chess_30231 = { character_event = { id = chess.12 days = 5 } } #Last move
				chess_30232 = { narrative_event = { id = chess.13 days = 1 } } #The end
			}
			clr_character_flag = chess_walk
			clr_character_flag = chess_30223
			clr_character_flag = chess_reveal
			clr_character_flag = chess_30225
			clr_character_flag = chess_30226
			clr_character_flag = chess_30227
			clr_character_flag = chess_30228
			clr_character_flag = chess_30229
			clr_character_flag = chess_30230
			clr_character_flag = chess_30231
			clr_character_flag = chess_30232
		}
	}
}

# chess.3 A stranger in the garden
# The ruler sees a stranger in simple garb: walk with them or not.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.3
	picture = GFX_evt_garden
	desc = EVTDESCchess3
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	hide_from = yes
	hide_new = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Have a chat
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess3_A

		random = { chance = 20 add_trait_gregarious_effect = yes }
		random = { chance = 30 remove_trait_shy_effect = yes }

		set_character_flag = walked_with_death
		set_character_flag = chess_30223
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Want to be alone
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess3_B

		random = { chance = 20 add_trait_shy_effect = yes }
		random = { chance = 30 remove_trait_gregarious_effect = yes }

		set_character_flag = chess_reveal
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}
}

# chess.4 The pilgrim of Morr
# The stranger is a priest of Morr asking for room and board: host, pay for
# the inn or turn away.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.4
	picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_daemon
		picture = GFX_evt_shadow
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_dawi }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_dawi
		picture = GFX_evt_monk
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_elf }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_elf
		picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { religion = FROM FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_A
		picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { religion = FROM FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_B
		picture = GFX_evt_monk
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = FROM } FROM = { is_woman_trigger = yes } }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_C
		picture = GFX_evt_wise_woman
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { religion = FROM } FROM = { is_woman_trigger = no } }
		text = EVTDESCchess4_D
		picture = GFX_evt_monk
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Come stay with me
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess4_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess4_A_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group }
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon
					has_character_flag = chess_voice_dawi
					has_character_flag = chess_voice_elf
				}
			}
			remove_trait_zealous_effect = yes
			add_trait = sympathy_old_world_group
		}

		set_character_flag = invited_death
		set_character_flag = chess_reveal
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Have some money, sleep at the inn
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess4_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess4_B_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.1 min = -15 }

		set_character_flag = gave_death_money
		set_character_flag = chess_reveal
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Go away!
	option = {
		name = { trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group } text = EVTOPTchess4_C_A }
		name = { trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group } } text = EVTOPTchess4_C_B }

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess4_C_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }

			piety = -50

			random = { chance = 50 remove_trait_zealous_effect = yes }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } }
			random = { chance = 50 add_trait_greedy_effect = yes }
		}
		random = { chance = 50 remove_trait_charitable_effect = yes }

		set_character_flag = chess_reveal
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}
}

# chess.5 For Whom The Bells Toll
# Death reveals itself with a dagger: challenge it to chess or fight.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
narrative_event = {
	id = chess.5
	title = EVTNAMEchess5
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_shadow

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon } text = EVTDESCchess5_daemon }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_dawi } text = EVTDESCchess5_dawi }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_voice_elf } text = EVTDESCchess5_elf }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = invited_death } text = EVTDESCchess5_A }
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = invited_death }
			has_character_flag = walked_with_death
		}
		text = EVTDESCchess5_B
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOR = { has_character_flag = invited_death has_character_flag = walked_with_death }
		}
		text = EVTDESCchess5_C
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			remove_trait = fatigue
			remove_trait = on_pilgrimage
			remove_trait = nun
			remove_trait = monk
		}
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = seducer trait = seductress } same_sex = FROM }
			FROM = { add_trait = homosexual } #To "enable" seduction
		}
	}

	#Play chess with me!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess5_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess5_A_TOOLTIP }

		set_character_flag = chess_30226
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Do your worst!
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess5_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess5_B_TOOLTIP }

		random_list = {
			#You die
			80 = {
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM }

				hidden_tooltip = {
					FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
				}
			}
			#You survive
			20 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					trait = wounded
					trait = mangled
					trait = one_eyed
					trait = one_handed
				}
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess5_B_TOOLTIP_SURVIVE }
				set_character_flag = chess_30225
				hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
			}
		}

		clr_character_flag = walked_with_death
		clr_character_flag = invited_death
		clr_character_flag = gave_death_money
	}
}

# chess.6 A struggle
# The ruler fought Death off but is hurt.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.6
	picture = GFX_evt_bloody_tools
	desc = EVTDESCchess6
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			25 = { modifier = { factor = 0 trait = wounded } set_character_flag = give_wounded }
			25 = { modifier = { factor = 0 trait = mangled } set_character_flag = give_mangled }
			25 = { modifier = { factor = 0 trait = one_eyed } set_character_flag = give_one_eyed }
			25 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = one_handed }
				set_character_flag = give_one_handed
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess6_B

		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_character_flag
			give_wounded = { add_trait_wounded_effect = yes }
			give_mangled = { mangle_effect = yes }
			give_one_eyed = { add_trait_one_eyed_effect = yes }
			give_one_handed = { add_trait_one_handed_effect = yes }
		}

		clr_character_flag = give_wounded
		clr_character_flag = give_mangled
		clr_character_flag = give_one_eyed
		clr_character_flag = give_one_handed

		FROM = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = ROOT } }
	}
}

# chess.7 Black or white
# The game begins.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.7
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	desc = EVTDESCchess7
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		set_variable = { which = chess_score value = 0 }

		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = { has_character_flag = invited_death has_character_flag = gave_death_money }
			}
			change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 }
			clr_character_flag = invited_death
			clr_character_flag = gave_death_money
		}
		clr_character_flag = walked_with_death
	}

	#White
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess7_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess7_A_TOOLTIP }

		set_character_flag = picked_white

		set_character_flag = chess_30227
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Black
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess7_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess7_B_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
		set_character_flag = picked_black

		set_character_flag = chess_30227
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}
}

# chess.8 The opening
# Choose a strategy: defend, attack, cheat, a practised plan or patience.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.8
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = picked_black } text = EVTDESCchess7_BLACK }
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = picked_white } text = EVTDESCchess7_WHITE }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Play it safe
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess8_A

		random_list = {
			25 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
				set_character_flag = good_move
			}
			55 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_NEUTRAL_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = neutral_move
			}
			20 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_BAD_MOVE }
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = -1 } }
				set_character_flag = bad_move
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Play it risky
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess8_B

		random_list = {
			20 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GREAT_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = great_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 2 } }
			}
			40 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
			40 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_TERRIBLE_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = bad_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = -2 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Deceitful
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = deceitful
			NOT = { trait = schemer }
			NOT = { AND = { trait = strategist is_smart_trigger = yes } }
		}
		name = EVTOPTchess8_C
		tooltip_info = deceitful

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Master schemer
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = schemer }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_C
		tooltip_info = schemer

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Gamer option
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = gamer }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_D
		tooltip_info = gamer

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GREAT_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = great_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 2 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Strategist option
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = strategist }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_D
		tooltip_info = strategist

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Genius option
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = genius NOT = { trait = gamer } }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_E
		tooltip_info = genius

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GREAT_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = great_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 2 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Quick
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = quick NOT = { trait = gamer } }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_E
		tooltip_info = quick

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Shrewd
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = shrewd NOT = { trait = gamer } }
		name = EVTOPTchess8_E
		tooltip_info = shrewd

		random_list = {
			100 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30228
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}
}

		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_seduction }

		picture = GFX_evt_lovers
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_intimidation }
		text = EVTDESCchess11_INTIMIDATION
		picture = GFX_evt_impalement
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = chess_brag }
		text = EVTDESCchess11_BRAG
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = good_trick }
		text = EVTDESCchess11_GOOD_TRICK
		picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = bad_trick }
		text = EVTDESCchess11_BAD_TRICK
		picture = GFX_evt_drunk
	}

		clr_character_flag = good_trick

		#Randomness
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = -1 } }
				50 = { }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30231
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

		name = EVTOPTchess11_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess11_B_TOOLTIP }

		clr_character_flag = bad_trick

		death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
		}
	}
}

# chess.12 The endgame
# Death offers to return a piece for a life: a spouse, friend, lover or child.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
character_event = {
	id = chess.12
	picture = GFX_evt_court_physician
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue

	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
		text = EVTDESCchess12_GOOD
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
			check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 4 }
		}
		text = EVTDESCchess12_NEUTRAL
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 4 } } }
		text = EVTDESCchess12_BAD
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes ai = yes liege = { character = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = chess_sacrifice
			}
			random_friend = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes ai = yes liege = { character = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = chess_sacrifice
			}
			random_lover = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes ai = yes liege = { character = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = chess_sacrifice
			}
			random_child = {
				limit = { is_alive = yes ai = yes liege = { character = ROOT } }
				save_event_target_as = chess_sacrifice
			}
		}
	}

	#Trust my skill
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTchess12_A

		random_list = {
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOR = { trait = gamer is_smart_trigger = yes } }
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GREAT_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = great_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 2 } }
			}
			20 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 OR = { trait = gamer is_smart_trigger = yes } }
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
			50 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.6 OR = { trait = gamer is_smart_trigger = yes } }
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_NEUTRAL_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = neutral_move
			}
			30 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.67 OR = { trait = gamer is_smart_trigger = yes } }
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_BAD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = bad_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = -1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30232
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}

	#Offer lover/friend
	option = {
		trigger = { event_target:chess_sacrifice = { always = yes } }
		name = {
			trigger = { any_spouse = { character = event_target:chess_sacrifice } }
			text = EVTOPTchess12_B_A
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { any_friend = { character = event_target:chess_sacrifice } }
			text = EVTOPTchess12_B_B
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { any_lover = { character = event_target:chess_sacrifice } }
			text = EVTOPTchess12_B_C
		}
		name = {
			trigger = { any_child = { character = event_target:chess_sacrifice } }
			text = EVTOPTchess12_B_D
		}

		event_target:chess_sacrifice = { save_event_target_as = kill_sacrifice }

		random_list = {
			15 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GREAT_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = great_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 2 } }
			}
			35 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_GOOD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = good_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = 1 } }
			}
			35 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_NEUTRAL_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = neutral_move
			}
			15 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = CHESS_BAD_MOVE }
				set_character_flag = bad_move
				hidden_tooltip = { change_variable = { which = chess_score value = -1 } }
			}
		}

		set_character_flag = chess_30232
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chess.2 } } }
	}
}

# chess.13 Dealings with Death
# Checkmate. Win a few more years, or lose and die; a daemon may take the
# soul instead and leave the ruler alive, possessed.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Death. Fired by chess.2.
narrative_event = {
	id = chess.13
	title = EVTNAMEchess13
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_danse_macabre

	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon
			check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 }
		}
		text = EVTDESCchess13_GOOD_daemon
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
		}
		text = EVTDESCchess13_BAD_daemon
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
		text = EVTDESCchess13_GOOD
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } } }
		text = EVTDESCchess13_BAD
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
		name = EVTOPTchess13_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess13_A_TT }

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_modifier = deaths_gift } }
			add_character_modifier = { name = deaths_gift duration = -1 }
		}

		remove_disease_trait_effect = yes

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
		}
	}

	#The daemon takes the soul, and leaves the body to walk a while longer
	option = {
		trigger = {
			has_character_flag = chess_voice_daemon
			NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } }
		}
		name = EVTOPTchess13_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess13_C_TT }

		set_character_flag = chess_sold_soul
		add_trait = possessed
		if = { limit = { NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group } } piety = -200 }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:kill_sacrifice = { always = yes } }
			event_target:kill_sacrifice = {
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = { leave_court_effect = yes death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}

	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { which = chess_score value = 5 } } }
		name = EVTOPTchess13_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTchess11_B_TOOLTIP }

		death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:kill_sacrifice = { always = yes } }
			event_target:kill_sacrifice = {
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = FROM }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				leave_court_effect = yes
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
				death = { death_reason = death_missing }
			}
		}
	}
}

# chess.14 Death's gift

# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect

		has_disease_trigger = yes

		remove_character_modifier = deaths_gift

	}
}

###############################################################################
# Part 6: Immortal rivals and the immortal crisis (namespace immortal)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: immortal rulers.
#
#   immortal.1-15     Another immortal comes to kill the ruler: Ramiro the
#                     Estalian duellist, the cruel Uran, Aldebrand the Grail
#                     Knight, Torhild the Norscan shieldmaiden, or Ironhoof
#                     the horse
#   immortal.16-19    The vassals suspect the ruler cannot die
#
# Callers: the on_yearly_pulse random list (1, 16, 19) and on_yearly_pulse
# (17, 18; 18 restored, vanilla had it off).
# Was RIP.30300-30318 in rip_flavor_events.txt (immortal.N-30299); the
# empty spare event RIP.30400 is gone. The vanilla rivals were from films
# and history (the Highlander's Ramirez, Arthur, Incitatus) and the vanilla
# loc keys were placeholdername_N.
###############################################################################

namespace = immortal

###############################################################################
# The rival (immortal.1-15)
###############################################################################

# immortal.1 Rumours of an Immortal
# Another immortal is coming: Ramiro the Estalian duellist, the cruel Uran,
# Aldebrand the Grail Knight, Torhild the Norscan shieldmaiden, or Ironhoof
# the horse.
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random list.
narrative_event = {
	id = immortal.1
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	border = GFX_event_narrative_frame_war
	title = EVTNAMEimmortal1

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = immortal_rival_appeared
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					has_character_flag = immortal_rival_highlander
					has_character_flag = immortal_rival_evildude
					has_character_flag = immortal_rival_goodguy
					has_character_flag = immortal_rival_shieldmaiden
					has_character_flag = immortal_rival_horse
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				100 = { set_character_flag = immortal_rival_highlander }
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_virtous = yes }
					set_character_flag = immortal_rival_evildude
				}
				100 = {
					modifier = { factor = 2 is_wicked = yes }
					modifier = {
						factor = 2
						OR = {
							trait = lunatic_1
							trait = lunatic_2
							trait = lunatic_3
							trait = cruel
							trait = wroth
							trait = cannibal_trait
							trait = impaler
						}
					}
					set_character_flag = immortal_rival_goodguy
				}
				100 = {
					set_character_flag = immortal_rival_shieldmaiden
					modifier = { factor = 2 combat_rating = 3 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 combat_rating = 4 }
				}
				15 = {
					modifier = { factor = 15 any_courtier = { culture = horse } }
					modifier = { factor = 10 any_courtier = { culture = horse trait = immortal } }
					set_character_flag = immortal_rival_horse
				}
			}
		}
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_highlander }
		text = EVTDESCimmortal1_highlander
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_evildude }
		text = EVTDESCimmortal1_evildude
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_goodguy }
		text = EVTDESCimmortal1_goodguy
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_shieldmaiden }
		text = EVTDESCimmortal1_shieldmaiden
	}
	desc = { trigger = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_horse } text = EVTDESCimmortal1_horse }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = immortal
		NOT = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_appeared }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAimmortal1
		trigger = {
			NOR = {
				trait = craven
				trait = lunatic_1
				trait = lunatic_2
				trait = lunatic_3
				trait = cruel
				trait = wroth
				trait = cannibal_trait
				trait = impaler
			}
		}
		character_event = {
			id = immortal.2
			days = 180
			random = 100
			tooltip = IMMORTAL_RIVAL_COMING_TOOLTIP
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = craven }
		name = EVTOPTBimmortal1
		character_event = {
			id = immortal.2
			days = 180
			random = 100
			tooltip = IMMORTAL_RIVAL_COMING_TOOLTIP
		}
	}
	option = {
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				trait = lunatic_1
				trait = lunatic_2
				trait = lunatic_3
				trait = cruel
				trait = wroth
				trait = cannibal_trait
				trait = impaler
			}
		}
		name = EVTOPTCimmortal1
		character_event = {
			id = immortal.2
			days = 180
			random = 100
			tooltip = IMMORTAL_RIVAL_COMING_TOOLTIP
		}
	}
}

# immortal.2 The rival arrives
# The rival strikes: fight, rage, trick, persuade, debate or give up. A
# prepared ruler has traps and hidden guards.
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler, immortal_rival the rival. Fired by
# immortal.1.
character_event = {
	id = immortal.2
	picture = GFX_evt_end_of_days
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_highlander }

			save_event_target_as = immortal_rival

		character_event = { id = immortal.9 days = 1 } #clears flags in one spot, hidden event
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = immortal_rival_horse }
				if = { #horse councillor?
					limit = {
						any_courtier = {
							ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							culture = horse
							has_job_title = yes
						}
					}
					random_courtier = {
						limit = { ai = yes is_alive = yes culture = horse has_job_title = yes }
						save_event_target_as = immortal_horse_victim
						if = {
							limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
							antideath_effect = yes
							break = yes
						}
						death = {
							death_reason = death_execution_horses
							killer = event_target:immortal_rival
						}
					}
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #any horse?
					limit = {
						any_courtier = {
							ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							culture = horse
							NOT = { event_target:immortal_rival = { character = THIS } }
						}
					}
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							culture = horse
							NOT = { event_target:immortal_rival = { character = THIS } }
						}
						save_event_target_as = immortal_horse_victim
						if = {
							limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
							antideath_effect = yes
							break = yes
						}
						death = {
							death_reason = death_execution_horses
							killer = event_target:immortal_rival
						}
					}
					break = yes
				}
				if = { #backup victim
					limit = {
						any_courtier = {
							ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							OR = {
								has_job_title = yes
								is_close_relative = ROOT
								is_consort = ROOT
								is_married = ROOT
							}
						}
					}
					random_courtier = {
						limit = {
							ai = yes
							is_alive = yes
							OR = {
								has_job_title = yes
								is_close_relative = ROOT
								is_consort = ROOT
								is_married = ROOT
							}
						}
						save_event_target_as = immortal_horse_victim
						if = {
							limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
							antideath_effect = yes
							break = yes
						}
						death = {
							death_reason = death_execution_horses
							killer = event_target:immortal_rival
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #fight. berserker and craven get the same option with custom text
		name = EVTOPTimmortal2_fight
		trigger = {
			health = 2
			NOR = {
				is_incapable = yes
				trait = severely_injured
				trait = infirm
				trait = craven
				trait = berserker
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 5 } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_great } #Tooltip reuse FTW
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					84 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.3 days = 0 } }
					10 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.4 days = 0 } }
					5 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.5 days = 0 } }
					1 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.6 days = 0 } }
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 3 }
				NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 5 } }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_very_good }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					70 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.3 days = 0 } }
					15 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.4 days = 0 } }
					10 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.5 days = 0 } }
					5 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.6 days = 0 } }
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 1 }
				NOT = { combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 3 } }
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_good }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					60 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.3 days = 0 } }
					20 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.4 days = 0 } }
					15 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.5 days = 0 } }
					5 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.6 days = 0 } }
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					combat_rating_diff = { who = event_target:immortal_rival value = 1 }
					event_target:immortal_rival = { combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 1 } }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_even }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					20 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.3 days = 0 } }
					20 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.4 days = 0 } }
					20 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.5 days = 0 } }
					40 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.6 days = 0 } }
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:immortal_rival = { combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 1 } }
				NOT = {
					event_target:immortal_rival = { combat_rating_diff = { who = ROOT value = 3 } }
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = { text = holmgang_chances_poor }
			hidden_tooltip = {
				random_list = {
					5 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.3 days = 0 } }
					15 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.4 days = 0 } }
					20 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.5 days = 0 } }
					60 = { long_character_event = { id = immortal.6 days = 0 } }
				}
			}
			break = yes
		}
		if = {

				NOT = {

	option = { #persuade
		trigger = {
			is_incapable = no
			diplomacy = 18
			OR = {
				NOT = { calc_true_if = { amount = 2 intrigue = 18 stewardship = 18 learning = 18 } }
				OR = { has_education_diplomacy_trigger = yes has_lifestyle_diplomacy_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = diplomacy
		name = EVTOPTimmortal2_persuade
		character_event = { id = immortal.10 days = 0 }
	}
	option = { #trick
		trigger = {
			is_incapable = no
			intrigue = 18
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					calc_true_if = { amount = 2 diplomacy = 18 stewardship = 18 learning = 18 }
				}
				OR = { has_education_intrigue_trigger = yes has_lifestyle_intrigue_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = intrigue
		name = EVTOPTimmortal2_trick
		character_event = { id = immortal.11 days = 0 }
	}
	option = { #money power
		trigger = {
			is_incapable = no
			stewardship = 18
			OR = {
				NOT = { calc_true_if = { amount = 2 diplomacy = 18 intrigue = 18 learning = 18 } }
				OR = {
					has_education_stewardship_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_stewardship_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = stewardship
		name = EVTOPTimmortal2_guards
		character_event = { id = immortal.12 days = 0 }
	}
	option = { #debate
		trigger = {
			is_incapable = no
			learning = 18
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					calc_true_if = { amount = 2 diplomacy = 18 intrigue = 18 stewardship = 18 }
				}
				OR = {
					has_education_learning_trigger = yes
					has_lifestyle_learning_trigger = yes
					is_learned_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		tooltip_info = learning
		name = EVTOPTimmortal2_debate
		character_event = { id = immortal.13 days = 0 }
	}
}

# immortal.3 A clean kill
# The ruler wins easily (berserker and craven variants).
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler, immortal_rival the rival. Fired by
# immortal.2.
long_character_event = {
	id = immortal.3
	desc = EVTDESCimmortal3
	border = GFX_event_long_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	desc = { trigger = { trait = berserker } text = EVTDESCimmortal3_berserker }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = craven } text = EVTDESCimmortal3_craven }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = berserker trait = craven } }
		name = EVTOPTAimmortal3
		event_target:immortal_rival = { death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT } }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = craven }
		name = EVTOPTimmortal3_lucky
		event_target:immortal_rival = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = berserker }
		name = EVTOPTimmortal3_who
		event_target:immortal_rival = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
	}
}

# immortal.5 A bloody win
# Both are run through; the ruler survives.
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler, immortal_rival the rival. Fired by
# immortal.2.
long_character_event = {
	id = immortal.5
	desc = EVTDESCimmortal5
	border = GFX_event_long_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_melee

	desc = { trigger = { trait = berserker } text = EVTDESCimmortal3_berserker }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = craven } text = EVTDESCimmortal3_craven }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAimmortal5
		event_target:immortal_rival = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
		if = { limit = { can_be_maimed_trigger = yes } add_maimed_trait_effect = yes break = yes }
		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = craven }
		name = EVTOPTimmortal4_dying
		event_target:immortal_rival = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { can_be_maimed_trigger = yes }
			add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
			break = yes
		}
		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
	}
	option = {
		trigger = { trait = berserker }
		name = EVTOPTimmortal4_scratch
		event_target:immortal_rival = {
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes break = yes }
			death = { death_reason = death_duel killer = ROOT }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { can_be_maimed_trigger = yes }
			add_maimed_trait_effect = yes
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
			break = yes
		}
		add_trait_wounded_effect = yes
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTimmortal3_TT }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# The immortal crisis (immortal.16-19)
###############################################################################

# immortal.16 Suspicious vassals
# Vassals start to suspect the ruler cannot die. Starts the immortal crisis.
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = immortal.16
	desc = EVTDESCimmortal16
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble

	immediate = {
		most_popular_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				immortal = no
				NOT = { opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 } }
				intrigue = 12
			}
			save_event_target_as = immortal_suspector
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 75

	trigger = {
		immortal = yes
		age > 75
		OR = {
			religion_group = old_world_group
			religion_group = stromfels_group
			religion_group = halfling_gods_group
			religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
		}
		NOR = {
			has_character_flag = immortal_crisis
			has_character_flag = immortal_crisis_confirmed
			is_daemon_race_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 100 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 150 }

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 200 }

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 300 }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTA_CM_6000 #Sweet text reuse
		set_character_flag = immortal_crisis
		event_target:immortal_suspector = {
			opinion = { modifier = immortal_suspicions who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
	}
}

# immortal.17 Crisis cleanup
# Hidden. Ends the immortal crisis when it is over.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler. Fired by on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = immortal.17
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes

	trigger = {
		immortal = yes
		OR = { has_character_flag = immortal_crisis has_character_flag = immortal_crisis_confirmed }
		NOR = {
			any_vassal = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_hate } }
			any_vassal = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions } }
		}
	}

	option = {
		clr_character_flag = immortal_crisis
		clr_character_flag = immortal_crisis_confirmed
	}
}

# immortal.18 The crisis spreads
# Hidden. Each year of the crisis, one more vassal starts to suspect
# (restored, vanilla had it switched off).
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler. Fired by on_yearly_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = immortal.18
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes

	immediate = {
		random_vassal = {
			limit = {
				ai = yes
				immortal = no
				NOR = {
					opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions }
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_loyalty }
					has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_hate }
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = immortal_suspector
		}
	}

	trigger = {
		has_character_flag = immortal_crisis
		NOT = { has_character_flag = immortal_crisis_confirmed }
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			immortal = no
			NOR = {
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 50 }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_loyalty }
				has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_hate }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAimmortal3
		random_list = {
			90 = {
				event_target:immortal_suspector = {
					opinion = { modifier = immortal_suspicions who = ROOT years = 100 }
				}
			}
			10 = {
				event_target:immortal_suspector = {
					opinion = { modifier = immortal_loyalty who = ROOT years = 100 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# immortal.19 The vassals are sure
# The suspicious vassals now know and plot.
# Scope: ROOT is the immortal ruler. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random list.
character_event = {
	id = immortal.19
	desc = EVTDESCimmortal19
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble

	is_triggered_only = yes

	only_playable = yes
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 75

	trigger = {
		immortal = yes
		age > 75
		has_character_flag = immortal_crisis
		any_vassal = {
			ai = yes
			immortal = no
			has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions }
			intrigue = 16
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 100 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 150 }

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 200 }

		modifier = { factor = 2 age = 300 }
	}

	option = {
		name = CURSES
		set_character_flag = immortal_crisis_confirmed
		any_vassal = {
			limit = { has_opinion_modifier = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions } }
			hidden_effect = { remove_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = immortal_suspicions } }
			opinion = { modifier = immortal_hate who = ROOT years = 100 }
		}
		clr_character_flag = immortal_crisis
	}
}
